# Give Every Class Role a Home and a Name (#1202) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Close #1202 in nine PRs. Every class in `src/Service` and `src/Http` gets one role, and its folder and its name say which. A PHPStan rule, `ServiceRoleRule`, lands first in report-only mode. Each later PR moves one role's classes and switches that role's checks on. The last PR makes the rule fail on every role.
- **PR A** (A0–A3, `Refs #1202`): `ServiceRoleRule`, its two collectors, the `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` marker, the report-only wiring (`serviceRoleChecks: []`, `phpstan-service-roles.neon`, `composer roles`), the `services.yaml` exclude, the committed move scripts, `CLAUDE.md` and `docs/architecture.md` §10.
- **PR B** (B0–B2, `Refs #1202`): interfaces. 31 renamed to `…Interface` (the two `Exception/` markers to `…ExceptionInterface`, D-P3), and every interface with its same-module implementations moves into a folder named after it. This includes the strategy families (`Repair/` → `PageRepair/`, `Media/Source/` → `MediaCandidateSource/`, `Media/Provider/` → `EmbedProvider/`, `Scraper/Layer/` → `ScrapeLayer/`, `Ingest/Platform/` → `PlatformEntryRule/`, and the recognizers into `Slideshow/SlideshowRecognizer/`) and `Entity\Positioned` → `PositionedInterface`. Enforces `interfaceName`, `interfaceFolder`.
- **PR C** (C0–C9, `Refs #1202`): factories. 16 move to `Factory/`, 3 are renamed from `…Builder`, and eleven build-and-save services hand their construction to eleven new factories. Enforces `factoryName`, `factoryFolder`.
- **PR D** (D0–D3, `Refs #1202`): models in the modules `A`–`P`. They move to `Model/` and take the `Model` suffix. The two Backup models stop holding the header DTO. Enforces `modelName`, `modelShape`, `modelFolder`, `dtoShape`.
- **PR E** (E0–E1, `Refs #1202`): models in the modules `Q`–`Z`. It enforces nothing new: the ten per-call objects the overrides send to `Pass/` are reported under `modelHome` until PR F moves them.
- **PR F** (F0–F3, `Refs #1202`): per-call objects move to `Pass/` and static-only helpers to `Support/`. Enforces `modelHome`, `passHome`, `supportHome`.
- **PR G** (G0–G4, `Refs #1202`): services. Every stateless service becomes `final readonly`. Every stateful one either implements `ResetInterface` (four, two of which leaked admin settings changes into the worker) or carries `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (seven). Enforces `rootService`, `statefulService`.
- **PR H** (H0–H1, `Refs #1202`): four event listeners take the `Listener` suffix. Enforces `listenerName`, `handlerName`.
- **PR I** (I0–I2, `Closes #1202`): the rule reports every check unconditionally. The report-only wiring goes, and the docs lose their transitional sentences.

**Architecture:**
- **The rule is the move list.** `ServiceRoleRule` is a `Rule<CollectedDataNode>`, fed by two collectors:
  - `ServiceRoleClassCollector`: every class in `App\Service`, `App\Http` and `App\EventListener`, with the Dto names its body mentions.
  - `ServiceRoleInstantiationCollector`: every `new` in production code, and whether it sits outside a constructor.
  
  For each class that is outside its role, the rule reports the check that caught it and ends the message with `Its home is <FQCN>.`
  
  A move PR does not hand-write its list:
  1. It runs the rule in report-only mode (`--error-format=json`).
  2. `derive-moves.php` keeps that PR's checks, applies `overrides.php` (the plan's judgment calls), and adds the tests that follow each class.
  3. `move-classes.php` does the moves.
  4. `compare-moves.php` proves that no code changed.
  5. `stale-names.php` turns the map into patterns, and a repo-wide `git grep` proves no old name is left outside `docs/`.
  
  The scripts are committed next to the plan copy, in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/`. The expected lists in Appendix A were re-derived at `2cd89be2` (the reconcile), so reviewers can diff them against what the run derived.
- **Report-only, then a ratchet.** `phpstan.dist.neon` registers the rule with `serviceRoleChecks: []` (it reports nothing). `phpstan-service-roles.neon` includes it and lists all fifteen checks, and `composer roles` runs it. Each role PR adds its checks to `phpstan.dist.neon`, so they fail the build from then on. PR I drops the list.
- **The move script** is #1162's (#1161 A0 Step 3) with five additions:
  - A short name may change, and the declaration, the bare uses and the comments of every file that named the class bare follow it.
  - All old names are rewritten in one pass, longest first. A new name that starts with an old one is therefore never rewritten twice (`SearchIndexReader` → `SearchIndexReader\SearchIndexReaderInterface`).
  - A moved file's `dirname(__DIR__, n)` and `__DIR__ . '/../…'` follow its new depth.
  - Every tracked file outside `docs/` follows the moves, not just `src`, `tests` and `config` (D16): `docker/`, `backend/bin/`, `backend/migrations/`, `backend/infection.json5`, the tool configs and `.github/`. FQCNs are rewritten in both spellings, `App\Service\X` and the doubled `App\\Service\\X` of JSON5 and quoted PHP strings.
  - `CLAUDE.md` and `docs/architecture.md` follow the moves, both FQCNs and `Service/…/X.php` paths.
- **Splits (PR C).** A build-and-save service with real construction logic hands the construction to a `…Factory`, and keeps the unit of work. "Build-and-save verdicts" gives every candidate the issue lists a recorded verdict.

**Tech Stack:** PHP 8.4, Symfony 7.4 (autowiring over `src/` with an `exclude`, `ResetInterface`/`kernel.reset`, Messenger's per-message reset), Doctrine ORM 3, PHPStan 2.x (`Collector`, `CollectedDataNode`, `RuleTestCase::getCollectors()`, `ReflectionProvider`), nikic/php-parser 5, PHPUnit 12, PHPMD codesize, phptramp, Infection.

**Spec:** GitHub issue #1202 (`gh issue view 1202`). The local copy is `scratchpad/issue-roles.md`, and every ruling in it is final. Also:
- memory `service-model-folder-and-suffix.md`;
- CLAUDE.md, "PHP code style — Clean Code is mandatory";
- `docs/architecture.md` §7–§9.

## Design decisions

- **D1: roles are read off names, never file paths.** Three facts are all the rule works from:
  - `module`: `App\Service\<Module>`, or `App\<Layer>` outside Service (#1161 D1).
  - `role`: the innermost of `Model`, `Dto`, `Factory`, `Pass`, `Support`, `Exception`, `Message`, `Handler` in the namespace.
  - `area`: the namespace before its first role segment.
  
  The fixture file can therefore hold every case in one file, as the other rule fixtures do.
- **D2: a class is "built per call"** when production code (`App\…` outside `App\Tests\…`) constructs it with `new` outside a constructor, or in a constructor while it holds mutable state. `new` inside a constructor of a stateless helper is composition (`TrailingBlankRemover`, `RecipeFactsRecognizer`), not a per-call object. A root class built per call is misfiled:
  - it goes to `Pass/` when it is mutable, holds a collaborator, or is named `…Pass`/`…Context`/`…Tally`;
  - otherwise it is data and goes to `Model/`, with the suffix.
- **D3: a collaborator** is:
  - a vendor class or interface that is not a value (DateTime, DateTimeZone, DateInterval, Throwable, DOM, `Dom\`);
  - an application interface outside `Model/`;
  - an application class that is not built per call and declares a public instance method.
  
  `GrafanaEnvDefaults` declares no method, so the overview model that holds it holds data.
- **D4: an interface's folder** is named after it without `Interface`. If its current folder holds nothing but the interface and its implementations, that folder is renamed in place (`Reader/Repair/` → `Reader/PageRepair/`). Otherwise a subfolder is made (`Reader/Slideshow/SlideshowRecognizer/`). An implementation of several same-module interfaces may sit in any of their folders, and the rule suggests the first in name order.
- **D5: `Exception/` wins like `Factory/` and `Model/`, and its interfaces carry the role.** A marker interface that exceptions implement stays flat in `Exception/` and is named `…ExceptionInterface` (`ProviderReplyFailureExceptionInterface`, `PasskeySignInFailureExceptionInterface`), as a `Factory/` one is `…FactoryInterface` and a `Model/` one `…ModelInterface`. `interfaceName` checks all three (D-P3 amended).
- **D6: scope.**
  - The interface checks and the `Factory`/`Model` name checks cover `src/Service` and `src/Http`.
  - The `Model`/`Dto`/`Pass`/`Support` placement and the service shape checks cover `src/Service` only. `src/Http`'s static `…Json` mappers are that layer's own role and stay in its area roots.
  - The listener check covers `src/EventListener`.
  - `src/Entity`, `Enum`, `Repository` and `Doctrine` are out of scope. The one exception is `Positioned` → `PositionedInterface`, which the issue names; it is renamed in place.
  - Doctrine mapping is unaffected: no entity moves, and `config/packages/doctrine.yaml` maps `src/Entity` only.
- **D7: the build-and-save threshold.** Split when the service's construction does any of these:
  - derives or normalises a value (hashing, encoding, sealing, a column guard, locale or name derivation, a policy-driven status, a rendered payload);
  - calls three or more setters after `new`;
  - builds related entities together.
  
  A `new` from values that are already valid, plus at most two setters, stays. An upsert that applies setters to a row it found *or* created is an update and stays.
- **D8: DRY across the splits.** Three places build a tag the same way (`TagEditor`, `BulkSubscriber`, `RestoreLoadPass`), and three build a feed (`SubscriptionCreator`, `BulkSubscriber`, `RestoreLoadPass`). The two factories live with their entity's module: `Tag/Factory/TagFactory` and `Feed/Factory/FeedFactory` (`Service/Feed` holds `FeedScheduler` and `OrphanedFeedReclaimer` since #1161). The new edges `Subscription → Tag`, `Backup → Tag`, `Subscription → Feed` and `Backup → Feed` close no cycle, which `ServiceModuleCycleRule` would otherwise fail: at `2cd89be2`, `Tag` reaches only `Ordering`, and `Feed` reaches only `Fetch`, `Crypto` and `Url`.
- **D9: renamed factories keep their method names** (`DigestMailFactory::build()`, `EsmtpTransportFactory::from()`). The issue renames classes. `EsmtpTransportBuilder` was static, so it also becomes an injected instance: a static-only class belongs in `Support/`, and a `…Factory` must sit in `Factory/`.
- **D10: moved files keep their comments** (the #1162 D6 / #1161 D7 precedent). The #1171 comment sweep takes the moved files. Where a moved comment now names a class by its old short name, the move script renames it with the class. Two exceptions:
  - A split rewrites the methods it moves and keeps their docblocks verbatim, including the long OAuth and passkey security docblocks. They document invariants a reader would otherwise break, and #1171 decides their length.
  - New files carry comments only at the CLAUDE.md bar.
- **D11: tests follow their class.** Every `tests/<mirror>/<Short>*Test.php` in the class's old test folder moves with it, and a `…Test` suffix follows a rename (`ReadScopeTest` → `ReadScopeModelTest`). A test named after an interface keeps its name when only `Interface` is appended (`CompletionStreamHeartbeatWiringTest`): it tests the family or its wiring, not the interface. A longer sibling class name claims its own tests: `EntryIngestorCategoriesTest` stays with `EntryIngestor`, not `Entry`. `tests/PhpStan` is never rewritten: its fixtures name classes on purpose.
- **D12: a stateful service's verdict follows what its state means.**
  - State that belongs to one request or one unit of work gets `ResetInterface`, so the Messenger worker drops it between messages.
  - State that must outlive messages on purpose gets `#[ProcessLifetimeState('<why>')]`.
  - `Monolog\ResettableInterface` counts as a reset: the logger's `kernel.reset` resets its handlers.
  
  See "Stateful services".
- **D13: role PRs switch their checks on.** A PR that empties a check adds it to `serviceRoleChecks` in the same PR, so the role cannot regress while later PRs are open. Enforcement ratchets:
  - PR B: `interfaceName`, `interfaceFolder`.
  - PR C: `factoryName`, `factoryFolder`.
  - PR D: `modelName`, `modelShape`, `modelFolder`, `dtoShape`.
  - PR E: nothing new (the override classes are still reported under `modelHome`).
  - PR F: `modelHome`, `passHome`, `supportHome`.
  - PR G: `rootService`, `statefulService`.
  - PR H: `listenerName`, `handlerName`.
- **D14: all commits are `refactor(#1202): …`**, the plan copy and the scripts included.
- **D15: the report is deterministic, so the derived move list is reproducible.**
  - PHPStan orders a run's errors by file, line and message, so the JSON report's order does not depend on the worker count.
  - Each message is a pure function of the collected facts: `ServiceRoleMap` sorts its classes by name, `sameModuleInterfaces()` sorts, and every "first folder" or "first interface" a message names comes from a sorted list.
  - `derive-moves.php` sorts the map it writes by class name.
  - A2 Step 3 and B1 Step 1 prove it: a second report run derives byte-identical output.
- **D16: a move reaches every tracked file outside `docs/`** (planner ruling, from #1161 PR A: `docker/php/worker-healthcheck.php` kept a stale import and the dev worker went unhealthy, and no gate noticed).
  - `move-classes.php` rewrites old FQCNs in every tracked text file outside `docs/` and `backend/tests/PhpStan/`, plus `docs/architecture.md`.
  - It handles the doubled spelling explicitly: `infection.json5`'s mutator `ignore` entries read `'App\\Service\\X::method'`, and a stale entry silently stops ignoring, so the moved file's mutants would fail `infection:diff`.
  - `stale-names.php` turns a map into one pattern per old FQCN, in both spellings, for `git grep -E -f`. Every move PR's Finishing runs it over the repository outside `docs/` (it must print nothing), and restarts the worker and waits for `docker compose ps` to report it healthy.
  - `docs/` keeps its old names on purpose: the committed plans are history.

## Build-and-save verdicts

Every candidate the issue lists, judged against D7 at `264fd6fe` and re-judged at `2cd89be2`. Only `BulkSubscriber` and `RestoreEntryLoader` changed shape in between (#1166 PR C); both verdicts stand, and C7 and C8 quote the merged code:

| Candidate | What it builds | Verdict | Task |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Ai/AiProviderConfigurator` | `AiProviderSettings`: seals the key, derives the hint; the copy derives its name, seals again, copies two settings | **Split** → `Ai/Factory/AiConfigurationFactory` | C2 |
| `Auth/ActionTokenService` | `ActionToken` from a hash and an expiry | **Stays.** `consume()` computes the same hash. A factory would split one invariant, "stored hash = lookup hash", across two classes | — |
| `Auth/BootstrapAdminProvisioner` | find-or-create `User`, then three setters | **Stays.** An upsert: the setters apply to a found row as much as a new one; the construction is `new User($email, $now)` | — |
| `Auth/RegistrationService` | `User`: normalises the locale, hashes the password, applies the policy's status | **Split** → `Auth/Factory/SignupUserFactory`; `verifyEmail()` moves to `Auth/EmailVerifier` and `resetPassword()` reuses `PasswordResetter::setPassword()` so the constructor stays under PHPMD's 10 (D-P7) | C3 |
| `Backup/RestoreEntryLoader` | `stateFor()`: `EntryState` from a line: read mark, three conditional marks, a clock fallback. Since #1166 C the constructor takes a `RestoreDestination` last and `begin()` is gone; `stateFor()` is unchanged and is the only reader of `$clock` | **Split** → `Backup/Factory/RestoredEntryStateFactory`; the loader swaps `ClockInterface` for the factory | C8 |
| `Backup/RestoreLoadPass` | `Tag` (3 setters), `Feed` (6), `Subscription` (5 + tags); `SavedSearch` (1) | **Split** → `Backup/Factory/RestoredFoundationFactory` (tag, feed, subscription), over `TagFactory` and `FeedFactory`; the saved search stays | C8 |
| `Catalog/CatalogCategoryEditor` | `CatalogCategory` + 3 setters | **Split** → `Catalog/Factory/CatalogCategoryFactory` | C6 |
| `Catalog/CatalogFeedEditor` | `CatalogFeed` + 6 setters | **Split** → `Catalog/Factory/CatalogFeedFactory` | C6 |
| `Catalog/CatalogImporter` (now `CatalogImportPass`, #1166 B) | `CatalogCategory` + 1 setter; plain `CatalogFeed` | **Stays** | — |
| `Ingest/EntryCategoryWriter` | plain `Category`, plain `EntryCategory` | **Stays** | — |
| `Ingest/EntryIngestor` | already split by #1166 B into `IngestedEntryFactory` | **Moves** → `Ingest/Factory/IngestedEntryFactory` | C1 |
| `Mail/MailDeliveryHealth` | plain `MailSendFailure` | **Stays** | — |
| `Mail/Settings/MailSettings` | empty `MailServerSettings`, then `apply()` | **Stays** (upsert) | — |

Factories under another name (issue): `DigestMailBuilder` → `DigestMailFactory` (builds an `Email`), `DigestPageBuilder` → `DigestPageFactory` (a `DigestPage`), `EsmtpTransportBuilder` → `EsmtpTransportFactory` (an `EsmtpTransport`). ## Stateful services

Every root or interface-folder service in `src/Service` with a mutable property at `2cd89be2` (named by its path after PR B). #1166 C's `BulkSubscribeBatch` and `BulkSubscribePositions` are mutable but per-call, so F1 moves them to `Pass/` and they are not services:

| Service | State | Leaks between worker messages? | Verdict (G2–G4) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Settings/InstanceSettings` | `$memoisedSettings`, the row | **Yes.** The worker keeps the row it first read until it restarts. The worker's EntityManager is cleared after every message, but the memo keeps the detached row | `ResetInterface` (G2) |
| `Settings/PublicBaseUrl/ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl` | `$resolved`, the base URL | **Yes.** `SendDueDigests` runs in the worker and builds digest links from it: an admin's new public URL reaches digests only after a restart | `ResetInterface` (G2) |
| `Recommendation/Run/TickLockKeepalive` | the held lock, its resource, the throttle, the loss flag | No: the advancer arms it and disarms it in a `finally` | `ResetInterface`, `reset()` → `release()` (G3): per-tick state |
| `Recommendation/Run/SweepStreamHeartbeat` (in `Run` since #1161) | the sweeping kind, the throttle | No: both sweeps disarm it in a `finally` | `ResetInterface`, `reset()` → `sweepEnded()` (G3): per-sweep state |
| `Grafana/EffectiveGrafanaSettings` | `$memoised`, the snapshot | By design: a per-process memo over a shared cache, pinned by `EffectiveGrafanaSettingsTest` (#1159) | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Search/EntryIndexer` | `$configured` | By design: the index settings are pushed once per process | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Logging/RequestIdProvider` | `$requestId` | No: `RequestIdListener` starts a new id for every request and every worker message | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Mail/DeferredMailer` | `$queued` | No: drained on `kernel.terminate`. A reset would drop queued mail | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Mail/Transport/DynamicMailTransport` | the cached transport and its settings signature | No: the signature is re-checked on every send | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Passkey/PasskeyCeremony` | the two ceremony managers, the serializer | No: library machinery built from no setting; `host()` reads the relying party on every call | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Profiling/ProfileSampler/ExcimerSampler` | the running profiler | By design: `WorkerProfilingListener` samples the worker across messages and flushes on its own interval | `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` (G4) |
| `Logging/Loki/LokiPushHandler` | `$buffer` | No: `Monolog\ResettableInterface`; the logger's reset calls its `reset()`, which flushes | No change (D12) |
| `Subscription/OwnedTagsCache`, `Subscription/SubscriptionTagPositions` | per-request caches | No | Already `ResetInterface` |

The two leaks are the behaviour changes of this issue. Both PR G's body and the "Wire changes" section list them.

A marker interface in `Exception/` is named `…ExceptionInterface`: `ProviderReplyFailure` → `ProviderReplyFailureExceptionInterface` and `PasskeySignInFailure` → `PasskeySignInFailureExceptionInterface`. The rule's `interfaceName` check requires the role suffix before `Interface` in every role folder (`Factory`, `Model`, `Exception`). **Applied:** D5, A1 (`InterfacePlacement`, `RoleFolderNames`, fixture lines 509 and 516, deletion checks 10 and 11), A3 §10, B2, Appendix A-B.
- **D-P6 ACCEPTED with a constraint:** `OidcBoundaryTest` is neither deleted nor weakened. Only its class-name literals change, and every assertion stays. The PR body says so in one line. **Applied:** D2 Step 4 (the literal, plus the comment and the `dirname()` depth that follow the file) and PR D's body.
- **D-P8 ACCEPTED with a constraint:** the two behaviour-changing resets (`InstanceSettings`, `ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl`) go in their own commit in PR G. A functional test drives the real `services_resetter` (kernel reset between two Messenger messages), not a direct `reset()` call, and proves the second message sees the changed setting. Its deletion check removes `ResetInterface` from the class. The PR body lists the behaviour change. **Applied:** G2.
- **Deletion checks:** each task implementer runs every deletion check and quotes its FAIL output in the task report, and the reviewer re-runs at least one. **Applied:** Global Constraints, every deletion-check step (each names one edit and quotes its FAIL), and every Finishing review.
- **Move scope is the whole repository, not src/tests/config** (from #1161 PR A, where `docker/php/worker-healthcheck.php` kept a stale `App\Service\Worker\…` import and the dev worker went unhealthy; nothing in the gates caught it). The move script rewrites FQCNs in every tracked file outside `docs/`, including `docker/`, `backend/bin/`, `backend/infection.json5` (its mutator ignore lists name `App\Service\…` methods: a stale entry silently stops ignoring, and the moved file's mutants then fail infection:diff), `phpmd`/`phpstan`/`phptramp` configs and `.github/`. Every move PR ends with a repo-wide stale-name grep (`git grep -n -E` over the old FQCNs, excluding `docs/`) that must print nothing, and restarts the worker and checks `docker compose ps` shows it healthy. **Applied:** D16, A0 Step 4 (`move-classes.php`, `stale-names.php`), and the Finishing sections of PRs B, C, D, E, F and H.
- **The report is deterministic.** **Applied:** D15, A2 Step 3, B1 Step 1.

## Planner decisions

All ruled; the plan is written as ruled.

- **D-P1 (slideshow): RULED, accepted** (B2, Appendix A-B). The recognizers go to `Reader/Slideshow/SlideshowRecognizer/`, and `Slideshow/` itself keeps its eleven other classes.
- **D-P2 (`BodyCleaningStep`): RULED, accepted** (B2, Appendix A-B). Its 15 implementations move to `Reader/BodyCleaning/BodyCleaningStep/`, because the module is `Reader` (#1161 D1).
- **D-P3 (`Exception/` interfaces): RULED, amended** (see "Planner rulings"): `…ExceptionInterface`, flat in `Exception/`.
- **D-P4 (two interfaces, one class): RULED, accepted** (B1 Step 2). `MeilisearchIndex` sits in `SearchIndexReader/`; `SearchIndexWriter/` holds only its interface.
- **D-P5 (`src/Http` scope): RULED, accepted** (D6). Only interface folders and `Factory` naming apply there.
- **D-P6 (OIDC): RULED, accepted with a constraint** (D2 Step 4). `IdToken` → `Oidc\Model\IdTokenModel`, `IdTokenClaims` → `Oidc\Model\IdTokenClaimsModel`, and `TokenEndpoint` and `IdTokenVerifier` → `Oidc\Pass\`, their code unchanged.
- **D-P7 (`RegistrationService`): RULED, accepted** (C3). `verifyEmail()` becomes `Auth/EmailVerifier`, and `resetPassword()` calls `PasswordResetter::setPassword()`; the constructor goes from 9 to 8.
- **D-P8 (`InstanceSettings`, `ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl`): RULED, accepted with a constraint** (G2). The worker sees an admin's settings change at its next message.
- **D-P9 (`CurlSmtpTransport`): RULED, accepted** (Appendix A-F). It goes to `Mail/Transport/Pass/`.
- **D-P10 (scripts): RULED, accepted** (A0 Step 4). The scripts are committed in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/` and run from `backend/`.
- **D-P11 (model PR split): RULED, accepted** (PRs D and E): `A`–`P` 149 moved files, `Q`–`Z` 154 (155 before PR A's execution rulings), both under the ~300 bar at `2cd89be2`.
- **D-P12 (listener names): RULED, accepted** (H1): append `Listener`.

## Not in scope

- `src/Entity`, `Enum`, `Repository`, `Doctrine`, `Security`, `Command`, `Controller`, `Dto`: they are outside the rule. `Positioned` is renamed in place (issue).
- Turning static helpers into services where they are policy: #1169 (issue, "Order"). F2 moves every static-only class to `Support/`, and #1169 moves the policy ones out again.
- Comment trimming in moved files: #1171 (D10).
- A message-name check (imperative, no suffix) and an event-name check (past tense): neither is mechanical. `handlerName` pins the handler side.
- Merging `SearchIndexReaderInterface` and `SearchIndexWriterInterface` (D-P4).
- The `EntryState`, `RecommendationRun`, `RecommendationRunLog` and `UserPasskey` `AutowireWrongClass` suppressions: they sit on entities, and `src/Entity` stays registered.

## Wire changes and deploy notes

- **Wire:** none. No route, response body, status, header, message class or stored value changes. Only container service ids change (an id is its FQCN).
- **Behaviour (PR G, D-P8):** the worker now drops the memoised instance settings and the resolved public base URL after every message.
- **Deploy (PR E):** `Version\LatestRelease` becomes `Version\Model\LatestReleaseModel`. `GitHubLatestReleaseReader` caches the object serialised in the `github.release.cache` pool, and an entry written before the deploy unserialises to `__PHP_Incomplete_Class`. The reader then reports no release until the entry expires. Clear that pool on deploy: `bin/console cache:pool:clear github.release.cache`. PR E's body says so, and no code changes. No other store keeps a moved class serialised: the OAuth, passkey, login-code and refresh stores write arrays, and the Grafana cache writes `toCacheEntry()`.

## Global Constraints

- **Paths and commands are relative to `backend/`**, except steps marked "from the repository root", `docs/…` and `CLAUDE.md`. The scripts are run as `php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/<script>.php`.
- **Behaviour does not change**, except PR G's two resets (D-P8). A move changes only `namespace` lines, `use` lines, FQCNs, renamed short names and the relative paths D11 names. Prove it per move task with the comment-stripped comparison in its steps.
- **Clean Code (CLAUDE.md) is mandatory:**
  - names reveal intent;
  - `final readonly` by default;
  - three parameters at most (constructors aside), and no boolean flag parameters;
  - guard clauses over nesting;
  - no `new` on a collaborator inside a method;
  - queries live in `src/Repository`;
  - typed exceptions in `Service/*/Exception`.
- **PHP 8.4 syntax pdepend 2.16.2 cannot parse is banned in `src`:** no `public private(set)`, and `new Foo()->bar()` must be written `(new Foo())->bar()`. `composer md` runs pdepend.
- **Every touched `src` file is PHPMD-clean** under `composer md`. Fix the design, never the threshold, and add no suppression.
- **phptramp:** no chain of 4+ hops across 2+ classes forwarding an unread parameter. `composer tramp` runs in `composer check`; if only the tramp step fails in CI, run `composer show larspohlmann/phptramp` first.
- **PHPStan at level max:** no new baseline entry and no `@phpstan-ignore`. Run `bin/console cache:clear && bin/console cache:warmup` after every move, because service ids change.
- **PhpStorm inspections** (`mcp__phpstorm__lint_files`) on every changed PHP file: ERROR and WARNING block. After a move:
  - an "unused import" warning in a file the script touched: delete the import and note it;
  - an import block the script rewrote in place is not re-sorted (#1162 precedent).
- **Comments:** default none; at most three lines; D10 for moved files.
- **PSR-12, 120 columns.**
- **Tests:**
  - PHPUnit 12 attributes;
  - `assertSame`, never `assertEquals` on values;
  - persisted ids through `requireId()`;
  - `$this->never()`, not `self::never()` (the EA inspection warns on `self::never()`, per the #1166 PR B carry-forward).
- **Every new test or pin gets a deletion check** that only its one edit makes fail. The step names the exact edit and quotes the expected FAIL line (PHPUnit's `Failed asserting that …` or the PHPStan message). Assert presence before absence, and never let an expected value be one the broken code also produces (`null`, `0`, `''`, `false`, an entity default, or `strpos()`'s `false`): such a check passes with the code gone. The task implementer runs each check, restores it by hand, and quotes its FAIL output in the task report; the reviewer re-runs at least one per PR and quotes it too.
- **Move scope is the repository (D16).** A move task never hand-edits an FQCN the script could have rewritten. It stages with `git add -A -- . ../docker ../.github ../CLAUDE.md ../docs/architecture.md` (from `backend/`), and every move PR's Finishing runs the stale-name grep and the worker health check.
- **Infection:** an escaped mutant on a touched line gets a killing test in the task that owns it. Never an `ignore`, never a lower `minMsi`. Renamed files are outside `infection:diff`'s `--git-diff-filter=AM`.
- **Gates for every task:** its tests, `composer check`, `composer md`, `bin/console lint:container`, and the PhpStorm inspections.
- **Gates per PR (Finishing):**
  - `php bin/phpunit` (SQLite);
  - `docker compose exec php composer test` (MySQL): check the containers are current first, and never run two MySQL legs at once;
  - `composer check`, `composer md` and `composer infection:diff`;
  - today's dev log scan;
  - for a move PR (B, C, D, E, F, H): the repo-wide stale-name grep and the worker health check.
- **Commits:** `refactor(#1202): <lower-case summary>`, one per task (a move task may commit the script run and its fix-ups separately), with no attribution lines.
- **PR bodies:** PRs A–H say `Refs #1202`. Neither their bodies nor any commit on their branches may contain "close", "closes", "closed", "fix", "fixes", "fixed", "resolve", "resolves" or "resolved" in any form. The Finishing steps grep for it. `MissingFaviconResolver`, `resolveFor` and "fixture" do not match the word-boundary check. PR I's body says `Closes #1202`.
- **Branches**, each cut from `origin/develop` after the previous PR merges:
  - `refactor/1202-service-role-rule`
  - `refactor/1202-interface-folders`
  - `refactor/1202-factories`
  - `refactor/1202-models-a-to-p`
  - `refactor/1202-models-q-to-z`
  - `refactor/1202-pass-and-support`
  - `refactor/1202-stateful-services`
  - `refactor/1202-listeners`
  - `refactor/1202-harden-role-rule`
- **Merge:** use the Monitor tool with one command, `gh pr checks <PR> --watch --fail-fast`. When it exits 0, run `gh pr merge <PR> --merge`. Never pass `--auto`.
- **The checkout is shared.** Run `git status --short` and `git branch --show-current` before any `switch`, `reset` or `stash`. Work in place, with no worktrees. Restore a deletion check by hand with the Edit tool, never with `git checkout --`.

# PR A — `ServiceRoleRule`, report-only

### Task A0: Preflight, branch, plan copy and the move scripts

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-class-roles.md` (the plan copy)
- Create: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/derive-moves.php`, `move-classes.php`, `compare-moves.php`, `stale-names.php`, `overrides.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/derive-moves.php <roles.json> <check,check> <map.php> ['<class regex>']`: prints `N classes, M tests` and a markdown table, and writes the map.
  - `php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/move-classes.php <map.php>`: prints `Moved … classes (… renamed); rewrote names in … files; added … imports in … files; renamed bare names in … files.`
  - `php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/compare-moves.php <map.php>`: prints each moved file whose code tokens differ from its old self, then `N of M moved files differ in code.`
  - `php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/stale-names.php <map.php>`: prints one POSIX ERE per old FQCN and one per old path reference (`Service/…/X.php`, a test's `tests/…/XTest.php`), for `git grep -E -f`.
  
  Every move PR's Finishing runs this block, from the repository root, with each map the PR moved (`<map>` stands for `interfaces`, `factories`, `models-a-p`, `models-q-z`, `pass`, `support` or `listeners`):
  ```bash
  php docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/stale-names.php backend/var/refactor-1202/<map>.php > backend/var/refactor-1202/<map>.stale
  git grep -l -E -f backend/var/refactor-1202/<map>.stale origin/develop -- backend/src backend/tests | wc -l
  git grep -n -E \( -f backend/var/refactor-1202/<map>.stale \) --and --not -e '^namespace ' -- . ':!docs' ':!backend/tests/PhpStan'
  git grep -n -E \( -f backend/var/refactor-1202/<map>.stale \) --and --not -e '^namespace ' -- docs/architecture.md
  ```
  The `--not` keeps a moved file's own `namespace` line out: an old FQCN can be exactly a new namespace (`…\SearchIndexReader` becomes the folder of `…\SearchIndexReader\SearchIndexReaderInterface`). The parentheses matter: without them `--and` binds only to the file's last pattern.
  Expected: the first grep prints a number above 0 (the patterns match the old names on develop, so they are not broken), and the other two print nothing. A hit is an old name the script left behind: rewrite it by hand and report the file.
  
  Then the worker health check, from the repository root:
  ```bash
  docker compose exec php bin/console cache:clear
  docker compose restart worker
  ```
  Wait with the Monitor tool until `docker compose ps worker --format '{{.Health}}'` prints `healthy` (start period 60 s, interval 60 s: allow four minutes), then run `docker compose exec -T worker php /usr/local/bin/worker-healthcheck.php; echo "exit $?"`. Expected: `healthy`, then `exit 0`. `unhealthy`, or a PHP fatal error naming an `App\…` class, is a stale import: find it with the grep above.

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

// Judgment calls on top of ServiceRoleRule's homes, read by derive-moves.php: class => [check, home].
// A null home keeps the class where it is. Each entry names the PR check that moves it; see the plan's "Overrides".

return [
    // Per-call objects the rule reads as data: each holds one call's input and is used within that call.
    'App\\Service\\Fetch\\PageUrls' => ['passHome', 'App\\Service\\Fetch\\Pass\\PageUrls'],
    'App\\Service\\Scraper\\CardFields' => ['passHome', 'App\\Service\\Scraper\\Pass\\CardFields'],
    'App\\Service\\Reader\\LeadingFurniture' => ['passHome', 'App\\Service\\Reader\\Pass\\LeadingFurniture'],
    'App\\Service\\Reader\\Media\\Sibling\\SiblingSearch' => [
        'passHome',
        'App\\Service\\Reader\\Media\\Sibling\\Pass\\SiblingSearch',
    ],
    'App\\Service\\Parser\\FeedMediaNode' => ['passHome', 'App\\Service\\Parser\\Pass\\FeedMediaNode'],
    'App\\Service\\ReaderAudit\\AuditReportHtml' => ['passHome', 'App\\Service\\ReaderAudit\\Pass\\AuditReportHtml'],
    'App\\Service\\ReaderAudit\\AuditFindingsFile' => [
        'passHome',
        'App\\Service\\ReaderAudit\\Pass\\AuditFindingsFile',
    ],
    'App\\Service\\Backup\\RestoreDestination' => ['passHome', 'App\\Service\\Backup\\Pass\\RestoreDestination'],
    // The Source/ folder dissolves into MediaCandidateSource/; its one non-source class joins Media's models.
    'App\\Service\\Reader\\Media\\Source\\ScannedPage' => [
        'modelHome',
        'App\\Service\\Reader\\Media\\Model\\ScannedPageModel',
    ],
    // The issue renames Ingest/Platform/ to PlatformEntryRule/; the rule collection it also held moves to Ingest/.
    'App\\Service\\Ingest\\Platform\\PlatformEntryRule' => [
        'interfaceName',
        'App\\Service\\Ingest\\PlatformEntryRule\\PlatformEntryRuleInterface',
    ],
    'App\\Service\\Ingest\\Platform\\RedditEntryRule' => [
        'interfaceFolder',
        'App\\Service\\Ingest\\PlatformEntryRule\\RedditEntryRule',
    ],
    'App\\Service\\Ingest\\Platform\\PlatformEntryRules' => [
        'interfaceFolder',
        'App\\Service\\Ingest\\PlatformEntryRules',
    ],
    // The test trait the sources' tests share follows them out of Source/.
    'App\\Tests\\Service\\Reader\\Media\\Source\\FindsMediaInRawPage' => [
        'interfaceFolder',
        'App\\Tests\\Service\\Reader\\Media\\MediaCandidateSource\\FindsMediaInRawPage',
    ],
    // Factories under another name (#1202): each builds and returns an object, so it takes the suffix.
    'App\\Service\\Mail\\Digest\\DigestMailBuilder' => [
        'factoryName',
        'App\\Service\\Mail\\Digest\\Factory\\DigestMailFactory',
    ],
    'App\\Service\\Mail\\Digest\\DigestPageBuilder' => [
        'factoryName',
        'App\\Service\\Mail\\Digest\\Factory\\DigestPageFactory',
    ],
    'App\\Service\\Mail\\Transport\\EsmtpTransportBuilder' => [
        'factoryName',
        'App\\Service\\Mail\\Transport\\Factory\\EsmtpTransportFactory',
    ],
    // Named by the issue although App\Entity is outside the rule: renamed in place.
    'App\\Entity\\Positioned' => ['interfaceName', 'App\\Entity\\PositionedInterface'],
];

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/derive-moves.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

// php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/derive-moves.php \
//     var/refactor-1202/roles.json <check,check,...> var/refactor-1202/<map>.php ['<class regex>']
// Reads a report-only ServiceRoleRule run (phpstan --error-format=json), keeps the given checks and the classes the
// regex matches, applies overrides.php, adds each moved class's tests, and writes the map move-classes.php reads.

const HOME = '/^Service role "(?<check>\w+)": (?<class>App\\\\[\w\\\\]+) .*Its home is (?<home>App\\\\[\w\\\\]+)\.$/s';

function namespaceOf(string $class): string
{
    return substr($class, 0, (int) strrpos($class, '\\'));
}

function shortNameOf(string $class): string
{
    return substr($class, (int) strrpos($class, '\\') + 1);
}

function pathOf(string $class): string
{
    $relative = str_starts_with($class, 'App\\Tests\\')
        ? 'tests/' . substr($class, strlen('App\\Tests\\'))
        : 'src/' . substr($class, strlen('App\\'));

    return str_replace('\\', '/', $relative) . '.php';
}

function testNamespaceOf(string $class): string
{
    return 'App\\Tests\\' . substr(namespaceOf($class), strlen('App\\'));
}

function fail(string $message): never
{
    fwrite(STDERR, $message . "\n");
    exit(1);
}

/** @return array<string, string> class => home, for the given checks and classes */
function reportedHomes(string $reportFile, array $checks, string $classPattern): array
{
    $report = json_decode((string) file_get_contents($reportFile), true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
    $homes = [];
    foreach ($report['files'] ?? [] as $file) {
        foreach ($file['messages'] as $message) {
            if (1 !== preg_match(HOME, $message['message'], $match)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (in_array($match['check'], $checks, true) && 1 === preg_match($classPattern, $match['class'])) {
                $homes[$match['class']][$match['home']] = true;
            }
        }
    }
    $overrides = require __DIR__ . '/overrides.php';
    foreach ($overrides as $class => [$check, $home]) {
        // An override decides both where the class goes and in which PR, whatever the report said.
        unset($homes[$class]);
        if (null !== $home && in_array($check, $checks, true) && 1 === preg_match($classPattern, $class)) {
            $homes[$class] = [$home => true];
        }
    }
    $moves = [];
    foreach ($homes as $class => $candidates) {
        if (1 !== count($candidates)) {
            fail(sprintf('Two homes for %s: %s', $class, implode(', ', array_keys($candidates))));
        }
        $moves[$class] = (string) array_key_first($candidates);
    }

    return $moves;
}

/** @return list<string> the short names of the classes declared next to $class */
function siblingsOf(string $class): array
{
    return array_map(
        static fn (string $file): string => basename($file, '.php'),
        glob(dirname(pathOf($class)) . '/*.php') ?: [],
    );
}

/** @return array<string, string> test class => its name after the class's own short name ("Test", "WiringTest") */
function testsOf(string $class): array
{
    $short = shortNameOf($class);
    $directory = dirname('tests/' . substr(pathOf($class), strlen('src/')));
    $longer = array_filter(
        siblingsOf($class),
        static fn (string $sibling): bool => strlen($sibling) > strlen($short) && str_starts_with($sibling, $short),
    );
    $tests = [];
    foreach (glob($directory . '/' . $short . '*Test.php') ?: [] as $file) {
        $testShort = basename($file, '.php');
        $rest = substr($testShort, strlen($short));
        $claimedByALongerSibling = array_filter(
            $longer,
            static fn (string $sibling): bool => str_starts_with($testShort, $sibling),
        );
        if (('Test' === $rest || ctype_upper($rest[0])) && [] === $claimedByALongerSibling) {
            $tests[testNamespaceOf($class) . '\\' . $testShort] = $rest;
        }
    }

    return $tests;
}

/**
 * A test follows its class and a model's new suffix; a test named after an interface tests its family or its wiring,
 * so it keeps its name when only `Interface` was appended.
 *
 * @param array<string, string> $moves
 */
function withTests(array $moves): array
{
    $all = $moves;
    foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
        $testedName = shortNameOf($new) === shortNameOf($old) . 'Interface' ? shortNameOf($old) : shortNameOf($new);
        foreach (testsOf($old) as $test => $rest) {
            $all[$test] = testNamespaceOf($new) . '\\' . $testedName . $rest;
        }
    }
    ksort($all);

    return $all;
}

/** @param array<string, string> $moves */
function assertNoCollision(array $moves): void
{
    $targets = [];
    foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
        if (isset($targets[$new])) {
            fail(sprintf('%s and %s both move to %s', $targets[$new], $old, $new));
        }
        $targets[$new] = $old;
        if (is_file(pathOf($new)) && !isset($moves[$new])) {
            fail(sprintf('%s would overwrite %s', $old, pathOf($new)));
        }
    }
}

[, $reportFile, $checkList, $mapFile] = $argv;
$classPattern = $argv[4] ?? '/./';
$moves = withTests(reportedHomes($reportFile, explode(',', $checkList), $classPattern));
assertNoCollision($moves);
file_put_contents($mapFile, "<?php\n\nreturn " . var_export($moves, true) . ";\n");

$sources = array_filter(array_keys($moves), static fn (string $class): bool => !str_starts_with($class, 'App\\Tests\\'));
printf("%d classes, %d tests\n\n| Class | Moves to |\n|---|---|\n", count($sources), count($moves) - count($sources));
foreach ($sources as $class) {
    printf("| `%s` | `%s` |\n", $class, $moves[$class]);
}
```

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/move-classes.php` is #1162's script (#1161 A0 Step 3) with the five additions of "Architecture". It reads and writes every tracked file outside `docs/` (D16), so a file outside `src`/`tests`/`config` that names a moved class, such as `docker/php/worker-healthcheck.php`, `infection.json5` or a migration's docblock, follows the move:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

// php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/move-classes.php var/refactor-1202/<map>.php
// The map returns array<string, string>: old FQCN => new FQCN, for src and test classes alike. #1162's script (#1161
// A0 Step 3) plus: a short name may change, names are rewritten in one pass, a moved file's relative paths follow it,
// and every tracked file outside docs/ follows the moves, with docs/architecture.md (#1202 D16).

const TYPE_TAGS = '@(?:phpstan-|psalm-)?(?:param|return|var|throws|extends|implements|use|mixin'
    . '|template(?:-covariant|-contravariant)?|assert(?:-if-true|-if-false)?|property(?:-read|-write)?'
    . '|method|self-out|this-out|require-extends|require-implements)\b';

function pathOf(string $class): string
{
    $relative = str_starts_with($class, 'App\\Tests\\')
        ? 'tests/' . substr($class, strlen('App\\Tests\\'))
        : 'src/' . substr($class, strlen('App\\'));

    return str_replace('\\', '/', $relative) . '.php';
}

function namespaceOf(string $class): string
{
    $separator = strrpos($class, '\\');

    return false === $separator ? '' : substr($class, 0, $separator);
}

function shortNameOf(string $class): string
{
    $separator = strrpos($class, '\\');

    return false === $separator ? $class : substr($class, $separator + 1);
}

function run(string $command): void
{
    passthru($command, $status);
    if (0 !== $status) {
        fwrite(STDERR, "Failed: {$command}\n");
        exit(1);
    }
}

/**
 * Every tracked text file in the repository, as a path relative to backend/, where this script runs. Never docs/ (the
 * committed plans are history) and never a PHPStan rule or fixture (their class names are test data).
 *
 * @return list<string>
 */
function repositoryFiles(): array
{
    $files = [];
    foreach (explode("\0", (string) shell_exec('git -C .. ls-files -z')) as $path) {
        if ('' === $path || str_starts_with($path, 'docs/') || str_starts_with($path, 'backend/tests/PhpStan/')) {
            continue;
        }
        $relative = str_starts_with($path, 'backend/') ? substr($path, strlen('backend/')) : '../' . $path;
        if (is_file($relative) && !str_contains((string) file_get_contents($relative, length: 8000), "\0")) {
            $files[] = $relative;
        }
    }

    return $files;
}

/** @return list<string> every tracked PHP file: src and tests, and the scripts outside them (docker/, migrations/) */
function phpFiles(): array
{
    return array_values(array_filter(
        repositoryFiles(),
        static fn (string $file): bool => str_ends_with($file, '.php'),
    ));
}

/**
 * Where a file is once the moves are done: a moved class's new path, any other file where it is. A file that is not
 * its class's PSR-4 path (docker/php/worker-healthcheck.php, a migration) never moves.
 *
 * @param array<string, string> $moves
 */
function destinationOf(string $file, string $class, array $moves): string
{
    return pathOf($class) === $file && isset($moves[$class]) ? pathOf($moves[$class]) : $file;
}

function declaredNamespace(string $code): string
{
    return 1 === preg_match('/^namespace ([^;]+);$/m', $code, $match) ? $match[1] : '';
}

/** @return array<string, true> the short names a file imports, aliases included */
function importedNames(string $code): array
{
    preg_match_all('/^use (?!function |const )([\w\\\\]+)(?: as (\w+))?;$/m', $code, $matches, PREG_SET_ORDER);
    $names = [];
    foreach ($matches as $match) {
        $names[$match[2] ?? shortNameOf($match[1])] = true;
    }

    return $names;
}

/** @param list<mixed> $tokens */
function significantNeighbour(array $tokens, int $index, int $step): mixed
{
    for ($position = $index + $step; isset($tokens[$position]); $position += $step) {
        $token = $tokens[$position];
        if (is_array($token) && in_array($token[0], [T_WHITESPACE, T_COMMENT, T_DOC_COMMENT], true)) {
            continue;
        }

        return $token;
    }

    return null;
}

/** @param list<mixed> $tokens */
function isClassPosition(array $tokens, int $index): bool
{
    $previous = significantNeighbour($tokens, $index, -1);
    $next = significantNeighbour($tokens, $index, 1);
    $previousId = is_array($previous) ? $previous[0] : $previous;
    $memberOrDeclaration = [
        T_OBJECT_OPERATOR, T_NULLSAFE_OBJECT_OPERATOR, T_DOUBLE_COLON, T_FUNCTION, T_CONST,
        T_CLASS, T_INTERFACE, T_TRAIT, T_ENUM, T_NAMESPACE, T_GOTO,
    ];
    if (in_array($previousId, $memberOrDeclaration, true)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (T_CASE === $previousId) {
        return is_array($next) && T_DOUBLE_COLON === $next[0];
    }

    return ':' !== $next;
}

function typeExpressionAfter(string $text, int $offset): string
{
    $length = strlen($text);
    while ($offset < $length && ctype_space($text[$offset])) {
        ++$offset;
    }
    $depth = 0;
    $type = '';
    for (; $offset < $length; ++$offset) {
        $char = $text[$offset];
        if (0 === $depth && ctype_space($char)) {
            if (!str_ends_with($type, ':')) {
                break;
            }

            continue;
        }
        $depth += match ($char) {
            '<', '{', '(', '[' => 1,
            '>', '}', ')', ']' => -1,
            default => 0,
        };
        $type .= $char;
    }

    return $type;
}

/** @return list<string> */
function identifiersIn(string $type): array
{
    preg_match_all('/(?<![\w\\\\$-])[A-Za-z_]\w*(?![\w\\\\-])(?!\s*\??:(?!:))/', $type, $matches);

    return $matches[0];
}

/** @return list<string> class names a docblock uses as a type, an imported type's source, or a @see target */
function docblockNames(string $docblock): array
{
    $text = (string) preg_replace(['~^\s*/\*\*~', '~\*/\s*$~', '~^\s*\*~m'], ' ', $docblock);
    $names = [];
    preg_match_all('/' . TYPE_TAGS . '/', $text, $tags, PREG_OFFSET_CAPTURE);
    foreach ($tags[0] as [$tag, $offset]) {
        array_push($names, ...identifiersIn(typeExpressionAfter($text, $offset + strlen($tag))));
    }
    preg_match_all('/@(?:phpstan-|psalm-)?import-type\s+\w+\s+from\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)(?![\w\\\\])/', $text, $imports);
    preg_match_all('/(?:\{@see|@see|@uses)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)(?![\w\\\\])/', $text, $sees);

    return [...$names, ...$imports[1], ...$sees[1]];
}

/** @return array<string, true> bare names the code and its docblock types use as class names */
function referencedNames(string $code): array
{
    $tokens = token_get_all($code);
    $names = [];
    foreach ($tokens as $index => $token) {
        if (!is_array($token)) {
            continue;
        }
        if (T_DOC_COMMENT === $token[0]) {
            foreach (docblockNames($token[1]) as $name) {
                $names[$name] = true;
            }

            continue;
        }
        if (T_STRING === $token[0] && isClassPosition($tokens, $index)) {
            $names[$token[1]] = true;
        }
    }

    return $names;
}

function removeImport(string $code, string $class): string
{
    $trimmed = str_replace("use {$class};\n", '', $code);
    if ($trimmed === $code) {
        return $code;
    }

    return (string) preg_replace_callback(
        '/^(namespace [^;]+;\n)\n\n+/m',
        static fn (array $match): string => $match[1] . "\n",
        $trimmed,
    );
}

function addImport(string $code, string $class): string
{
    $line = "use {$class};\n";
    if (str_contains($code, "\n{$line}")) {
        return $code;
    }
    preg_match_all(
        '/^use (?!function |const )([\w\\\\]+)(?: as \w+)?;\n/m',
        $code,
        $imports,
        PREG_SET_ORDER | PREG_OFFSET_CAPTURE,
    );
    if ([] === $imports) {
        $namespaceEnd = (int) strpos($code, ";\n", (int) strpos($code, "\nnamespace ")) + 2;

        return substr_replace($code, "\n" . $line, $namespaceEnd, 0);
    }
    foreach ($imports as $import) {
        if (strcasecmp($import[1][0], $class) > 0) {
            return substr_replace($code, $line, $import[0][1], 0);
        }
    }
    $last = $imports[array_key_last($imports)];

    return substr_replace($code, $line, $last[0][1] + strlen($last[0][0]), 0);
}

/** @return array<string, string> short name => class, for the imports that carry no alias */
function importedClasses(string $code): array
{
    preg_match_all('/^use (?!function |const )([\w\\\\]+);$/m', $code, $matches);
    $classes = [];
    foreach ($matches[1] as $class) {
        $classes[shortNameOf($class)] = $class;
    }

    return $classes;
}

/**
 * One pass over every old name, longest first, so a new name that starts with an old one is never rewritten again.
 * A leading separator is kept; a name followed by a separator is a namespace, not the class, and stays.
 *
 * @param array<string, string> $names
 */
function rewriteNames(string $text, array $names, string $separator): string
{
    uksort($names, static fn (string $a, string $b): int => strlen($b) <=> strlen($a));
    $alternation = implode('|', array_map(static fn (string $name): string => preg_quote($name, '/'), array_keys($names)));
    $escapedSeparator = preg_quote($separator, '/');

    return (string) preg_replace_callback(
        '/(?<![\w\\\\])((?:' . $escapedSeparator . ')?)(' . $alternation . ')(?![\w\\\\])/',
        static fn (array $match): string => $match[1] . $names[$match[2]],
        $text,
    );
}

/**
 * Both spellings of every old FQCN: `App\Service\X` (PHP, YAML, neon, Markdown) and the doubled `App\\Service\\X` of
 * quoted PHP and JSON5 strings, such as infection.json5's `ignore` entries. Neither pass can match the other's
 * spelling: one needs a single backslash between segments, the other two.
 *
 * @param array<string, string> $moves
 */
function rewriteQualifiedNames(string $text, array $moves): string
{
    $doubled = [];
    foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
        $doubled[str_replace('\\', '\\\\', $old)] = str_replace('\\', '\\\\', $new);
    }

    return rewriteNames(rewriteNames($text, $moves, '\\'), $doubled, '\\\\');
}

/** @param array<string, string> $moves */
function rewriteDocumentPaths(string $text, array $moves): string
{
    foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
        if (str_starts_with($old, 'App\\Tests\\')) {
            continue;
        }
        $oldPath = substr(pathOf($old), strlen('src/'));
        $newPath = substr(pathOf($new), strlen('src/'));
        $text = (string) preg_replace_callback(
            '/(?<!\w)' . preg_quote($oldPath, '/') . '/',
            static fn (): string => $newPath,
            $text,
        );
    }

    return $text;
}

/** @param list<mixed> $tokens */
function isDeclaredName(array $tokens, int $index): bool
{
    $previous = significantNeighbour($tokens, $index, -1);

    return is_array($previous) && in_array($previous[0], [T_CLASS, T_INTERFACE, T_ENUM, T_TRAIT], true);
}

/** @param array<string, string> $renames old short name => new short name */
function renameInProse(string $text, array $renames): string
{
    $alternation = implode('|', array_map(static fn (string $name): string => preg_quote($name, '/'), array_keys($renames)));

    return (string) preg_replace_callback(
        '/(?<![\w\\\\$])(' . $alternation . ')(?!\w)/',
        static fn (array $match): string => $renames[$match[1]],
        $text,
    );
}

/** @param array<string, string> $renames old short name => new short name */
function renameBareNames(string $code, array $renames): string
{
    $tokens = token_get_all($code);
    $renamed = '';
    foreach ($tokens as $index => $token) {
        if (!is_array($token)) {
            $renamed .= $token;

            continue;
        }
        [$id, $text] = $token;
        $isName = T_STRING === $id && isset($renames[$text])
            && (isClassPosition($tokens, $index) || isDeclaredName($tokens, $index));
        if ($isName) {
            $renamed .= $renames[$text];

            continue;
        }
        $renamed .= in_array($id, [T_COMMENT, T_DOC_COMMENT], true) ? renameInProse($text, $renames) : $text;
    }

    return $renamed;
}

/** A moved file reaches the same places: `dirname(__DIR__, n)` and `__DIR__ . '/../…'` follow its new depth. */
function shiftRelativePaths(string $code, int $delta, string $file): string
{
    $code = (string) preg_replace_callback(
        '/(\\\\?dirname\(__DIR__)(?:,\s*(\d+))?\)/',
        static function (array $match) use ($delta): string {
            $levels = ('' === ($match[2] ?? '') ? 1 : (int) $match[2]) + $delta;

            return 1 === $levels ? $match[1] . ')' : $match[1] . ', ' . $levels . ')';
        },
        $code,
    );

    return (string) preg_replace_callback(
        "/__DIR__(\\s*\\.\\s*')\\/((?:\\.\\.\\/)*)/",
        static function (array $match) use ($delta, $file): string {
            $levels = intdiv(strlen($match[2]), 3) + $delta;
            if ('' === $match[2] || $levels < 1) {
                fwrite(STDERR, "Fix the __DIR__ path in {$file} by hand: it points inside the old folder\n");
                exit(1);
            }

            return '__DIR__' . $match[1] . '/' . str_repeat('../', $levels);
        },
        $code,
    );
}

function depthOf(string $path): int
{
    return substr_count(dirname($path), '/');
}

/** @var array<string, string> $moves */
$moves = require $argv[1];
$oldNamespaces = array_flip(array_map('namespaceOf', array_keys($moves)));
$renames = array_filter(
    $moves,
    static fn (string $new, string $old): bool => shortNameOf($new) !== shortNameOf($old),
    ARRAY_FILTER_USE_BOTH,
);

// Where every class of an affected namespace lives once the moves are done.
$destinations = [];
foreach (phpFiles() as $file) {
    $namespace = declaredNamespace((string) file_get_contents($file));
    $class = $namespace . '\\' . basename($file, '.php');
    if (isset($oldNamespaces[$namespace]) && pathOf($class) === $file) {
        $destinations[$namespace][basename($file, '.php')] = $moves[$class] ?? $class;
    }
}

// 1. Before anything moves: which bare names each file will have to import, and which renamed classes it names bare.
$importsFor = [];
$bareRenamesIn = [];
foreach (phpFiles() as $file) {
    $code = (string) file_get_contents($file);
    $namespace = declaredNamespace($code);
    $class = $namespace . '\\' . basename($file, '.php');
    $destination = $moves[$class] ?? $class;
    $destinationFile = destinationOf($file, $class, $moves);
    $imported = importedClasses($code);
    $importedShortNames = importedNames($code);
    foreach ($renames as $old => $new) {
        $short = shortNameOf($old);
        $namesIt = ($imported[$short] ?? null) === $old
            || (namespaceOf($old) === $namespace && !isset($importedShortNames[$short]));
        if ($namesIt) {
            $bareRenamesIn[$destinationFile][$short] = shortNameOf($new);
        }
    }
    if (!isset($oldNamespaces[$namespace]) || pathOf($class) !== $file) {
        continue;
    }
    foreach (array_keys(referencedNames($code)) as $name) {
        $target = $destinations[$namespace][(string) $name] ?? null;
        if (null === $target || isset($importedShortNames[$name]) || $target === $destination) {
            continue;
        }
        if (namespaceOf($target) !== namespaceOf($destination)) {
            $importsFor[$destinationFile][$target] = true;
        }
    }
}

// 2. Each file moves, and its namespace line and its relative paths with it.
foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
    $from = pathOf($old);
    $to = pathOf($new);
    if (!is_dir(dirname($to))) {
        mkdir(dirname($to), 0o775, true);
    }
    run(sprintf('git mv %s %s', escapeshellarg($from), escapeshellarg($to)));
    $code = str_replace(
        'namespace ' . namespaceOf($old) . ";\n",
        'namespace ' . namespaceOf($new) . ";\n",
        (string) file_get_contents($to),
    );
    if (dirname($from) !== dirname($to)) {
        $code = shiftRelativePaths($code, depthOf($to) - depthOf($from), $to);
    }
    file_put_contents($to, $code);
}

// 3. Both spellings of every old name, in every tracked file outside docs/ (code, strings, docblocks, config, JSON5,
// Docker, CI, migrations, Markdown) and in docs/architecture.md, become the new one.
$rewrittenFiles = 0;
foreach ([...repositoryFiles(), '../docs/architecture.md'] as $file) {
    $code = (string) file_get_contents($file);
    $isDocument = str_ends_with($file, '.md');
    if (!$isDocument && !str_contains($code, 'App\\')) {
        continue;
    }
    $rewritten = rewriteQualifiedNames($code, $moves);
    if ($isDocument) {
        $rewritten = rewriteDocumentPaths($rewritten, $moves);
    }
    if ($rewritten !== $code) {
        file_put_contents($file, $rewritten);
        ++$rewrittenFiles;
    }
}

// 4. An import of a class that now shares the file's namespace goes.
foreach (phpFiles() as $file) {
    $code = (string) file_get_contents($file);
    $namespace = declaredNamespace($code);
    $trimmed = $code;
    foreach ($moves as $new) {
        if (namespaceOf($new) === $namespace) {
            $trimmed = removeImport($trimmed, $new);
        }
    }
    if ($trimmed !== $code) {
        file_put_contents($file, $trimmed);
    }
}

// 5. A bare name that left the file's namespace gets an import.
$added = 0;
foreach ($importsFor as $file => $classes) {
    $code = (string) file_get_contents($file);
    foreach (array_keys($classes) as $class) {
        $code = addImport($code, (string) $class);
        ++$added;
    }
    file_put_contents($file, $code);
}

// 6. A renamed class is renamed where a file named it bare: its declaration, its uses, its comments.
foreach ($bareRenamesIn as $file => $shortRenames) {
    $code = (string) file_get_contents($file);
    $renamed = renameBareNames($code, $shortRenames);
    if ($renamed !== $code) {
        file_put_contents($file, $renamed);
    }
}

printf(
    "Moved %d classes (%d renamed); rewrote names in %d files; added %d imports in %d files; "
        . "renamed bare names in %d files.\n",
    count($moves),
    count($renames),
    $rewrittenFiles,
    $added,
    count($importsFor),
    count($bareRenamesIn),
);
```

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/compare-moves.php`, the proof that a move changed no code:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

// php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/compare-moves.php var/refactor-1202/<map>.php [<base ref>]
// A move changes no code: compares each moved file's code tokens with its old self at the base ref (default
// origin/develop), without comments, whitespace, the namespace line or the imports, and with the map applied to the
// old side's names. Prints each file that still differs, and where.

function pathOf(string $class): string
{
    $relative = str_starts_with($class, 'App\\Tests\\')
        ? 'tests/' . substr($class, strlen('App\\Tests\\'))
        : 'src/' . substr($class, strlen('App\\'));

    return str_replace('\\', '/', $relative) . '.php';
}

function shortNameOf(string $class): string
{
    return substr($class, (int) strrpos($class, '\\') + 1);
}

/** @return list<string> */
function codeTokens(string $code): array
{
    $kept = [];
    $depth = 0;
    $skipping = false;
    foreach (token_get_all($code) as $token) {
        $id = is_array($token) ? $token[0] : null;
        $text = is_array($token) ? $token[1] : $token;
        if (in_array($id, [T_COMMENT, T_DOC_COMMENT, T_WHITESPACE, T_OPEN_TAG], true)) {
            continue;
        }
        if ('{' === $text || T_CURLY_OPEN === $id || T_DOLLAR_OPEN_CURLY_BRACES === $id) {
            ++$depth;
        } elseif ('}' === $text) {
            --$depth;
        }
        if (0 === $depth && in_array($id, [T_NAMESPACE, T_USE], true)) {
            $skipping = true;

            continue;
        }
        if ($skipping) {
            $skipping = ';' !== $text;

            continue;
        }
        $kept[] = $text;
    }

    return $kept;
}

/**
 * @param array<string, string> $moves
 * @param array<string, string> $shortRenames
 *
 * @return \Closure(string): string what a token of the old file reads as after the moves
 */
function renamer(array $moves, array $shortRenames): \Closure
{
    $names = [];
    foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
        $names[$old] = $new;
        $names[str_replace('\\', '\\\\', $old)] = str_replace('\\', '\\\\', $new);
    }
    uksort($names, static fn (string $a, string $b): int => strlen($b) <=> strlen($a));
    $alternation = implode('|', array_map(static fn (string $name): string => preg_quote($name, '/'), array_keys($names)));
    $pattern = '/(?<![\w\\\\])(\\\\{0,2})(' . $alternation . ')(?![\w\\\\])/';

    return static function (string $token) use ($names, $pattern, $shortRenames): string {
        if (isset($shortRenames[$token])) {
            return $shortRenames[$token];
        }
        if (!str_contains($token, 'App\\')) {
            return $token;
        }

        return (string) preg_replace_callback(
            $pattern,
            static fn (array $match): string => $match[1] . $names[$match[2]],
            $token,
        );
    };
}

/** @var array<string, string> $moves */
$moves = require $argv[1];
$base = $argv[2] ?? 'origin/develop';
$shortRenames = [];
foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
    if (shortNameOf($old) !== shortNameOf($new)) {
        $shortRenames[shortNameOf($old)] = shortNameOf($new);
    }
}

$rename = renamer($moves, $shortRenames);
$differing = 0;
foreach ($moves as $old => $new) {
    $before = (string) shell_exec(sprintf('git show %s', escapeshellarg($base . ':backend/' . pathOf($old))));
    $oldTokens = array_map($rename, codeTokens($before));
    $newTokens = codeTokens((string) file_get_contents(pathOf($new)));
    if ($oldTokens === $newTokens) {
        continue;
    }
    ++$differing;
    $at = 0;
    while (($oldTokens[$at] ?? null) === ($newTokens[$at] ?? null)) {
        ++$at;
    }
    printf(
        "%s differs at token %d: '%s' became '%s'\n",
        pathOf($new),
        $at,
        implode(' ', array_slice($oldTokens, $at, 8)),
        implode(' ', array_slice($newTokens, $at, 8)),
    );
}

printf("%d of %d moved files differ in code.\n", $differing, count($moves));
```

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/stale-names.php`, the patterns for the repo-wide stale-name grep (D16):
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

// php ../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-1202-scripts/stale-names.php var/refactor-1202/<map>.php
// One POSIX ERE per old FQCN of the map, for `git grep -E -f`: it matches the name spelled with one backslash between
// segments (PHP, YAML, neon, Markdown) or two (quoted PHP strings, infection.json5). A name followed by a word
// character or a backslash is a longer name or a namespace, not this class, and does not match.

/** @var array<string, string> $moves */
$moves = require $argv[1];
$patterns = [];
foreach (array_keys($moves) as $old) {
    // Segments are [A-Za-z0-9_], never special in an ERE. The output reads `\\{1,2}` (one or two backslashes) between
    // segments, and `\\` inside the closing bracket, which POSIX reads as a literal backslash either way.
    $patterns[] = implode('\\\\{1,2}', explode('\\', $old)) . '([^[:alnum:]_\\\\]|$)';
}
sort($patterns);
echo implode("\n", $patterns), "\n";
```

`tests/PhpStan/ServiceRoleRuleTest.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\PhpStan;

use PhpParser\NodeFinder;
use PHPStan\Reflection\ReflectionProvider;
use PHPStan\Rules\Rule;
use PHPStan\Testing\RuleTestCase;

/** @extends RuleTestCase<ServiceRoleRule> */
final class ServiceRoleRuleTest extends RuleTestCase
{
    private const string FIXTURES = __DIR__ . '/data/service-role-fixtures.php';
    private const string SHOP = 'App\Service\Shop\\';
    private const string NEEDS_SUFFIX = 'is an interface, so its name ends in Interface';
    private const string OUTSIDE_ITS_FOLDER = 'sits outside the folder named after it';
    private const string NOT_READONLY = 'keeps no state, so it is readonly';
    private const string NOT_FINAL = 'is a service, so it is final';
    private const string TAKES_A_MODEL = 'takes App\Service\Shop\Model\Price, which the container cannot supply';
    private const string DATA_NOT_READONLY = 'is data, so it is final readonly';
    private const string WRONG_FACTORY_FOLDER = 'sits in the wrong Factory/ folder';
    private const string WRONG_MODEL_FOLDER = 'sits in the wrong Model/ folder';
    private const string LISTENER = 'App\EventListener\Fixtures\NotifyTheShop';

    /** @var list<string> */
    private array $checks = [];

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->checks = array_map(
            static fn (ServiceRoleCheck $check): string => $check->value,
            ServiceRoleCheck::cases(),
        );
    }

    protected function getRule(): Rule
    {
        return new ServiceRoleRule(self::getContainer()->getByType(ReflectionProvider::class), $this->checks);
    }

    protected function getCollectors(): array
    {
        return [new ServiceRoleClassCollector(new NodeFinder()), new ServiceRoleInstantiationCollector()];
    }

    public function testEveryClassOutsideItsRoleIsReportedWithItsHome(): void
    {
        $this->analyse([self::FIXTURES], [
            [

            [self::shop('passHome', 'CheckoutContext', 'is built per call', 'Pass\CheckoutContext'), 99],
            [self::shop('passHome', 'TapeCounter', 'is built per call', 'Pass\TapeCounter'), 114],
            [self::shop('rootService', 'Cashier', self::NOT_READONLY), 162],
            [self::shop('rootService', 'Porter', self::NOT_FINAL), 170],
            [self::shop('rootService', 'Register', self::TAKES_A_MODEL), 178],
            [
                self::shop(
                    'statefulService',

    public function testARunReportsOnlyTheChecksItIsGiven(): void
    {
        $this->checks = ['listenerName', 'rootService'];

        $this->analyse([self::FIXTURES], [
            [self::shop('rootService', 'Cashier', self::NOT_READONLY), 162],
            [self::shop('rootService', 'Porter', self::NOT_FINAL), 170],
            [self::shop('rootService', 'Register', self::TAKES_A_MODEL), 178],
            [self::listenerMessage(), 493],
        ]);
    }

    private static function roleSuffix(string $role): string
    {
        return sprintf('is an interface in %s/, so its name ends in %sInterface', $role, $role);
    }

    private static function listenerMessage(): string
    {
        return self::message(
            'listenerName',
            self::LISTENER,
            'is an event listener, so its name ends in Listener',
            self::LISTENER . 'Listener',
        );
    }

    private static function shop(string $check, string $class, string $problem, ?string $home = null): string
    {
        return self::message($check, self::SHOP . $class, $problem, null === $home ? null : self::SHOP . $home);
    }

    private static function message(string $check, string $class, string $problem, ?string $home = null): string
    {
        $message = sprintf('Service role "%s": %s %s.', $check, $class, $problem);

        return null === $home ? $message : $message . sprintf(' Its home is %s.', $home);
    }
}
```

What the fixtures pin, case by case:
- **Interfaces.**
  - `PriceSource` has no suffix and sits in a mixed folder: it gets a subfolder.
  - `Rules\DiscountRule` sits in a folder that holds only its family: the folder is renamed.
  - `Basket\` is settled, but `BasketTotals` sits in it without implementing the interface.
  - `Till\TillPriceSource` is a cross-module implementation, and stays.
  - `Exception\ShopFailure` is renamed `…ExceptionInterface` and stays flat (D-P3 amended).
  - `Stamp\Factory\StampSource` lacks the suffix entirely, and `Stamp\Model\InkInterface` has `Interface` but not the role before it: both are `interfaceName` errors, and neither is also a `factoryName` or `modelName` error.
- **Factories.** `ReceiptFactory` sits outside `Factory/`, and `InvoiceBuilder` sits inside it without the suffix. `Http\Fixtures\ResponseFactory` is in `src/Http`. `Crate\Factory` is flat and fine. `Label\Factory` mixes an interface family with a plain factory.
- **Models and DTOs.**
  - `CartModel` sits outside `Model/`, and `Price` sits inside it without the suffix.
  - `ShelfModel` is not readonly, `StockModel` takes a clock, and `OrderModel` names a DTO.
  - `OwnerModel` takes an entity, and `Currency` is an enum: both are fine.
  - `Dto\RefundLineModel` is named like a model, `LooseLine` is not readonly, and `LineFields` is static-only.
  - `Badge\Model` mixes a model interface with a plain model.
- **Root placement.**
  - `Aisle` is an enum, and `Rounding` is static-only.
  - `Discount` is data built per call.
  - `ScanProgress` is mutable. `PricedBasket` holds a collaborator. `CheckoutContext` is named for its role.
  - `TapeCounter` is mutable and built in a constructor. `Wrapper` is stateless, built in a constructor, and stays.
  - `Support\Clerk` is not static-only.
- **Services.** `Cashier` is not readonly, `Porter` is not final, and `Register` takes a model; its `#[Autowire]` and defaulted parameters do not count. `Memo` keeps state. `ResettingMemo` and `LifetimeMemo` are fine.
- **Messaging.** `RestockHandler` does not match its message, and `CountTillsHandler` does. `NotifyTheShop` needs the listener suffix, and `ShopListener` has it.
- **The run's own filter.** The second test gives the rule two checks, and exactly their errors come back.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to watch it fail**

Run: `php bin/phpunit tests/PhpStan/ServiceRoleRuleTest.php`
Expected: FAIL with `Class "App\Tests\PhpStan\ServiceRoleRule" not found` (or the collector class, whichever PHPUnit loads first).
