`src/Service/Catalog/Factory/CatalogFeedFactory.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Catalog\Factory;

use App\Entity\CatalogFeed;
use App\Repository\CatalogCategoryRepository;
use App\Repository\CatalogFeedRepository;
use App\Service\Catalog\CatalogFeedDetails;

final readonly class CatalogFeedFactory
{
    public function __construct(
        private CatalogCategoryRepository $categories,
        private CatalogFeedRepository $feeds,
    ) {
    }

    public function create(CatalogFeedDetails $details): CatalogFeed
    {
        $category = $this->categories->getById($details->categoryId);
        $feed = new CatalogFeed($category, $details->title, $details->url);
        $feed->setSiteUrl($details->siteUrl);
        $feed->setDescription($details->description);
        $feed->setSourceFormat($details->sourceFormat);
        $feed->setEnabled($details->enabled);
        $feed->setLocked($details->locked);
        $feed->setPosition($this->feeds->nextPositionInCategory($category->requireId()));

        return $feed;
    }
}
```

### Task C7: `TagFactory` and `FeedFactory`

Three places build a tag the same way, and three build a feed (D8). Re-judged against the code at `2cd89be2`, after #1166 C reshaped `BulkSubscriber` around a per-batch `BulkSubscribeBatch`:
- `TagEditor::create()` calls three setters after `new Tag`. Split.
- `BulkSubscriber::persistNewTag()` calls three setters after `new Tag` (the position comes from the batch's `BulkSubscribePositions`). Split.
- `BulkSubscriber::persistNewFeed()` calls `setSourceFormat()`, `setTitle()` and `scheduleNextFetchAt()` after `new Feed`. Split: the two setters go to the factory, and the scheduling stays with the service, since it is when the batch saves the row, not what the row is.
- `SubscriptionCreator` builds the identical feed with two setters. Under D7 it would stay, but D8 makes it the third copy, so it uses `FeedFactory` too.
- `BulkSubscriber::persistSubscription()` (`new` + one setter) stays. `RestoreLoadPass` joins in C8.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Service/Tag/Factory/TagFactory.php`, `src/Service/Feed/Factory/FeedFactory.php`
- Modify: `src/Service/Tag/TagEditor.php`, `src/Service/Subscription/BulkSubscriber.php`, `src/Service/Subscription/SubscriptionCreator.php`, `tests/Service/Subscription/SubscriptionServiceTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Service/Tag/Factory/TagFactoryTest.php`, `tests/Service/Feed/Factory/FeedFactoryTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `App\Service\Tag\Factory\TagFactory::create(User $user, TagDetails $details, int $position): Tag`. It has no constructor.
  - `App\Service\Feed\Factory\FeedFactory::create(string $url, string $sourceFormat, ?string $title): Feed`. It has no constructor.
  
  Neither factory persists. `BulkSubscriber` gains `TagFactory $tagFactory` and `FeedFactory $feedFactory` (7 → 9 parameters), `TagEditor` gains `TagFactory $tagFactory` (3 → 4), and `SubscriptionCreator` gains `FeedFactory $feedFactory` (6 → 7). No test builds `BulkSubscriber` or `TagEditor` with `new` at `2cd89be2`: `BulkSubscriberTest` and `BulkSubscriberTagLookupTest` take it from the container.

### Task C8: `RestoredFoundationFactory` and `RestoredEntryStateFactory`

Re-judged against the code at `2cd89be2`:
- `RestoreLoadPass` (unchanged by #1166 C) calls three, six and five setters for a tag (`loadTag()`), a feed (`createFeed()`) and a subscription (`loadSubscription()`). Split: the rows come from a factory that maps the backup's lines, over C7's `TagFactory` and `FeedFactory`. The pass keeps its work: the indexes, the held feeds, the counts, the tag links and the flushes. The saved search (one setter) stays.
- `RestoreEntryLoader` changed shape in #1166 C: the constructor now takes a `RestoreDestination` last and reads the user and the feed targets off it, and `begin()` is gone. `stateFor()` did not change: it restores a read mark, three conditional marks and a clock fallback, and it is the only reader of `$clock`. Split: `stateFor()`'s construction moves to a factory, and the loader swaps `ClockInterface` for it. `RestoreEntryLoaderFactory::create()`, which builds the loader and its destination, passes the factory on.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredFoundationFactory.php`, `src/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredEntryStateFactory.php`
- Modify: `src/Service/Backup/RestoreLoadPass.php`, `src/Service/Backup/RestoreLoader.php`, `src/Service/Backup/RestoreEntryLoader.php`, `src/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoreEntryLoaderFactory.php` (moved there by C1)
- Modify: `tests/Service/Backup/RestoreLoadPassTest.php`, `tests/Service/Backup/RestoreEntryLoaderTest.php`, `tests/Service/Backup/EntryPartRestorerTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredFoundationFactoryTest.php`, `tests/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredEntryStateFactoryTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `App\Service\Backup\Factory\RestoredFoundationFactory::__construct(TagFactory $tags, FeedFactory $feeds)`, with `tag(User $user, TagLine $line): Tag`, `feed(FeedLine $line): Feed` and `subscription(User $user, Feed $feed, SubscriptionLine $line): Subscription`. The subscription comes without its tags.
  - `App\Service\Backup\Factory\RestoredEntryStateFactory::__construct(ClockInterface $clock)`, with `create(User $user, Entry $entry, EntryStateLine $line): EntryState`.
  - `RestoreLoadPass::__construct(EntityManagerInterface $em, FeedRepository $feeds, SavedSearchSlug $slug, RestoredFoundationFactory $rows)`.
  - `RestoreLoader` gains `RestoredFoundationFactory $rows`.
  - `RestoreEntryLoader::__construct(EntityManagerInterface $em, EntryRepository $entries, EntryStateRepository $entryStates, EntryBatchInserter $inserter, EntryIndexer $indexer, RestoredEntryStateFactory $states, RestoreDestination $destination)`, and `RestoreEntryLoaderFactory` swaps `ClockInterface $clock` for `RestoredEntryStateFactory $states`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredFoundationFactoryTest.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Service\Backup\Factory;

use App\Entity\Feed;
use App\Entity\User;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\FeedLine;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\SubscriptionLine;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\TagLine;
use App\Service\Backup\Factory\RestoredFoundationFactory;
use App\Service\Feed\Factory\FeedFactory;
use App\Service\Tag\Factory\TagFactory;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class RestoredFoundationFactoryTest extends TestCase
{
    public function testATagIsRestoredAsTheFileStatesIt(): void
    {
        $tag = $this->factory()->tag($this->user(), new TagLine('Science', '#123456', 'flask', 4));

        self::assertSame('Science', $tag->getName());
        self::assertSame('#123456', $tag->getColor());
        self::assertSame('flask', $tag->getIcon());
        self::assertSame(4, $tag->getPosition());
    }

    public function testAFeedIsRestoredWithEveryFieldTheFileCarries(): void
    {
        $feed = $this->factory()->feed(new FeedLine(
            url: 'https://restored.example/feed.xml',
            siteUrl: 'https://restored.example',
            title: 'Restored',
            description: 'A restored feed',
            faviconUrl: 'https://restored.example/favicon.ico',
            imageUrl: 'https://restored.example/logo.png',
            sourceFormat: 'scraped',
        ));

        self::assertSame('https://restored.example/feed.xml', $feed->getUrl());
        self::assertSame('https://restored.example', $feed->getSiteUrl());
        self::assertSame('Restored', $feed->getTitle());
        self::assertSame('A restored feed', $feed->getDescription());
        self::assertSame('https://restored.example/favicon.ico', $feed->getFaviconUrl());
        self::assertSame('https://restored.example/logo.png', $feed->getImageUrl());
        self::assertSame('scraped', $feed->getSourceFormat());
    }

    public function testASubscriptionIsRestoredWithItsSettings(): void
    {
        $createdAt = new \DateTimeImmutable('2026-07-01 08:00:00');
        $readUntil = new \DateTimeImmutable('2026-07-15 09:30:00');

        $subscription = $this->factory()->subscription(
            $this->user(),
            new Feed('https://restored.example/feed.xml'),
            new SubscriptionLine(
                feedUrl: 'https://restored.example/feed.xml',
                customTitle: 'My name for it',
                position: 3,
                markedReadUntil: $readUntil,
                createdAt: $createdAt,
                tags: [],
                includeInAllItems: false,
                includeInForYou: false,
            ),
        );

        self::assertSame('My name for it', $subscription->getCustomTitle());
        self::assertSame(3, $subscription->getPosition());
        self::assertSame($readUntil, $subscription->getMarkedReadUntil());
        self::assertSame($createdAt, $subscription->getCreatedAt());
        self::assertFalse($subscription->isIncludeInAllItems());
        self::assertFalse($subscription->isIncludeInForYou());
    }

    private function factory(): RestoredFoundationFactory
    {
        return new RestoredFoundationFactory(new TagFactory(), new FeedFactory());
    }

    private function user(): User
    {
        return new User('restorer@example.test', new \DateTimeImmutable('2026-08-01'));
    }
}
```
Every expected value differs from the entity's default (`Subscription` defaults both flags to `true`, `Feed` its URLs and texts to `null` and its format to `'xml'`, `Tag` its position to 0). `Subscription` keeps the `DateTimeImmutable` instances it is given, so `assertSame` on them holds.

`tests/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredEntryStateFactoryTest.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Service\Backup\Factory;

use App\Entity\Entry;
use App\Entity\Feed;
use App\Entity\User;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\EntryStateLine;
use App\Service\Backup\Factory\RestoredEntryStateFactory;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use Symfony\Component\Clock\MockClock;

final class RestoredEntryStateFactoryTest extends TestCase
{
    private const string RESTORED_AT = '2026-08-20 00:00:00';

    public function testAFavoriteKeptViewedLineRestoresAllThree(): void
    {
        $viewedAt = new \DateTimeImmutable('2026-08-10 10:00:00');

        $state = $this->factory()->create($this->user(), $this->entry(), $this->line(true, true, $viewedAt));

        self::assertTrue($state->isFavorite());
        self::assertTrue($state->isKept());
        self::assertTrue($state->isViewed());
        self::assertEquals($viewedAt, $state->getViewedAt());
    }

    public function testAPlainLineRestoresNoMark(): void
    {
        $state = $this->factory()->create($this->user(), $this->entry(), $this->line(false, false, null, false));

        self::assertFalse($state->isFavorite());
        self::assertFalse($state->isKept());
        self::assertFalse($state->isViewed());
    }

    public function testAViewedLineWithoutItsTimeTakesTheRestoresOwn(): void
    {
        $state = $this->factory()->create($this->user(), $this->entry(), $this->line(false, false, null));

        self::assertEquals(
            new \DateTimeImmutable(self::RESTORED_AT, new \DateTimeZone('UTC')),
            $state->getViewedAt(),
        );
    }

    private function factory(): RestoredEntryStateFactory
    {
        return new RestoredEntryStateFactory(new MockClock(self::RESTORED_AT, 'UTC'));
    }

    private function user(): User
    {
        return new User('state-restorer@example.test', new \DateTimeImmutable('2026-08-01'));
    }

    private function entry(): Entry
    {
        $at = new \DateTimeImmutable('2026-08-01 12:00:00');

        return new Entry(new Feed('https://restored.example/feed.xml'), 'guid-1', null, 'An entry', $at, $at);
    }

    private function line(bool $favorite, bool $kept, ?\DateTimeImmutable $viewedAt, bool $viewed = true): EntryStateLine
    {
        return new EntryStateLine(
            feedUrl: 'https://restored.example/feed.xml',
            guidHash: hash('sha256', 'guid-1'),
            isHidden: false,
            isFavorite: $favorite,
            isKept: $kept,
            hiddenAt: null,
            isViewed: $viewed,
            viewedAt: $viewedAt,
        );
    }
}
```
The `$viewed` default keeps the three call sites short, and it is a test helper's value, not a behaviour switch. If the inspection flags the boolean parameters, split `line()` into `viewedLine()` and `plainLine()`. The restore time is built in UTC on both sides, as `MockClock` is, so the comparison does not depend on the machine's timezone.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the two factories**

`src/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredFoundationFactory.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Backup\Factory;

use App\Entity\Feed;
use App\Entity\Subscription;
use App\Entity\Tag;
use App\Entity\User;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\FeedLine;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\SubscriptionLine;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\TagLine;
use App\Service\Feed\Factory\FeedFactory;
use App\Service\Tag\Factory\TagFactory;
use App\Service\Tag\TagDetails;

/** The rows a backup's foundation lines describe, as the file states them. */
final readonly class RestoredFoundationFactory
{
    public function __construct(private TagFactory $tags, private FeedFactory $feeds)
    {
    }

    public function tag(User $user, TagLine $line): Tag
    {
        return $this->tags->create($user, new TagDetails($line->name, $line->color, $line->icon), $line->position);
    }

    public function feed(FeedLine $line): Feed
    {
        $feed = $this->feeds->create($line->url, $line->sourceFormat, $line->title);
        $feed->setSiteUrl($line->siteUrl);
        $feed->setDescription($line->description);
        $feed->setFaviconUrl($line->faviconUrl);
        $feed->setImageUrl($line->imageUrl);

        return $feed;
    }

    /** Without its tags: only the restore knows the tags this file declared. */
    public function subscription(User $user, Feed $feed, SubscriptionLine $line): Subscription
    {
        $subscription = new Subscription($user, $feed, $line->createdAt);
        $subscription->setCustomTitle($line->customTitle);
        $subscription->setPosition($line->position);
        $subscription->setMarkedReadUntil($line->markedReadUntil);
        $subscription->setIncludeInAllItems($line->includeInAllItems);
        $subscription->setIncludeInForYou($line->includeInForYou);

        return $subscription;
    }
}
```

`src/Service/Backup/Factory/RestoredEntryStateFactory.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Backup\Factory;

use App\Entity\BackedUpReadMark;
use App\Entity\Entry;
use App\Entity\EntryState;
use App\Entity\User;
use App\Service\Backup\Dto\EntryStateLine;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

final readonly class RestoredEntryStateFactory
{
    public function __construct(private ClockInterface $clock)
    {
    }

    public function create(User $user, Entry $entry, EntryStateLine $line): EntryState
    {
        $state = new EntryState($user, $entry);
        $state->restoreReadMark(new BackedUpReadMark($line->isHidden, $line->hiddenAt));
        if ($line->isFavorite) {
            $state->markFavorite();
        }
        if ($line->isKept) {
            $state->markKept();
        }
        if ($line->isViewed) {
            // A "viewed" line without its timestamp keeps the flag and takes the restore's own time (#307).
            $state->markViewed($line->viewedAt ?? $this->clock->now());
        }

        return $state;
    }
}
```

### Task C9: `RecommendationRunLogFactory`; enforce `factoryName` and `factoryFolder`

`RecommendationCallRecorder` derives the attempt number from the recorded rows and renders the request before it builds the run-log row. Both derivations move to a factory. The recorder keeps the persist and the flush, and still returns the `RecordedCall`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/Factory/RecommendationRunLogFactory.php`
- Modify: `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/RecommendationCallRecorder.php`, `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/RecommendationCallRecorderTest.php`, `phpstan.dist.neon`
- Test: `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/Factory/RecommendationRunLogFactoryTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `App\Service\Recommendation\Run\Factory\RecommendationRunLogFactory::__construct(RecommendationRunLogRepository $logs, ClockInterface $clock)`, with `create(RecommendationRun $run, CallSlot $slot, CompletionRequest $request): RecommendationRunLog` (not persisted).
  - `RecommendationCallRecorder::__construct(EntityManagerInterface $entityManager, RecommendationCallRepository $calls, ClockInterface $clock, RecommendationRunLogFactory $logFactory)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/Service/Recommendation/Run/Factory/RecommendationRunLogFactory.php`. The two helpers and their docblocks are the recorder's, verbatim:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Recommendation\Run\Factory;

use App\Entity\RecommendationRun;
use App\Entity\RecommendationRunLog;
use App\Repository\RecommendationRunLogRepository;
use App\Service\Ai\Completion\CompletionRequest;
use App\Service\Recommendation\Run\CallSlot;
use Symfony\Component\Clock\ClockInterface;

final readonly class RecommendationRunLogFactory
{
    public function __construct(
        private RecommendationRunLogRepository $logs,
        private ClockInterface $clock,
    ) {
    }

    public function create(RecommendationRun $run, CallSlot $slot, CompletionRequest $request): RecommendationRunLog
    {
        return new RecommendationRunLog(
            $run,
            $slot->phase,
            $slot->batchNumber,
            $this->nextAttempt($run, $slot),
            self::renderedRequest($request),
            $this->clock->now(),
        );
    }

    /** Derived from the rows already recorded, so the recorder cannot disagree with its own rows. */
    private function nextAttempt(RecommendationRun $run, CallSlot $slot): int
    {
        return $this->logs->countAttempts($run, $slot->phase, $slot->batchNumber) + 1;
    }

    /** Pretty-printed for the human the debug view exists for: the payload as sent, minus transport framing. */
    private static function renderedRequest(CompletionRequest $request): string
    {
        return json_encode(
            ['model' => $request->model, 'messages' => $request->messages],
            \JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | \JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | \JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | \JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR,
        );
    }
}
```
Use the recorder's own `ClockInterface` import: it is `Symfony\Component\Clock\ClockInterface` at `2cd89be2`.

In `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/RecommendationCallRecorder.php`:
- Replace the constructor with:

```php
        private EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
        private RecommendationCallRepository $calls,
        private ClockInterface $clock,
        private RecommendationRunLogFactory $logFactory,
    ) {
    }
```

```php
        $log = $this->logFactory->create($run, $slot, $request);
        $this->entityManager->persist($log);
        $this->entityManager->flush();
```
- Delete the methods `persistedLog()`, `nextAttempt()` and `renderedRequest()` with their docblocks.
- Imports: remove `App\Entity\RecommendationRunLog` and `App\Repository\RecommendationRunLogRepository`, and add `use App\Service\Recommendation\Run\Factory\RecommendationRunLogFactory;`.

In `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/RecommendationCallRecorderTest.php`, replace the `setUp()` statement `$this->recorder = new RecommendationCallRecorder(…);` with:
```php
        $this->recorder = new RecommendationCallRecorder(

            $this->clock,
            new RecommendationRunLogFactory($this->logs, $this->clock),
        );
```
and add `use App\Service\Recommendation\Run\Factory\RecommendationRunLogFactory;` in order.

### Task D1: `RestoreSourceModel`: the backup models stop holding the header DTO

`BackupInventory` and `RestorePreview` are models, and both hold the `BackupHeader` DTO. The issue rules that a model never imports a DTO, and that a DTO names a model only in a mapping method. So the header maps itself to the one model the preview shows, `Backup/Model/RestoreSourceModel`, and both models hold that instead. The wire does not change.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Service/Backup/Model/RestoreSourceModel.php`
- Modify: `src/Service/Backup/Dto/BackupHeader.php` (`toSource()`), `src/Service/Backup/BackupInventory.php`, `src/Service/Backup/RestorePreview.php`, `src/Service/Backup/BackupTally.php` (F moves it to `Pass/` later), `src/Service/Backup/RestorePreviewer.php`, `src/Http/RestorePreviewJson.php`
- Modify: `tests/Service/Backup/RestorePreviewerTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Service/Backup/Dto/BackupHeaderTest.php` (one test)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `App\Service\Backup\Model\RestoreSourceModel(string $backupId, ?int $parts, \DateTimeImmutable $createdAt, ?string $sourceUrl, ?string $sourceEmail)`, with public readonly fields.
  - `BackupHeader::toSource(): RestoreSourceModel`.
  - `BackupInventory::$source` and `RestorePreview::$source` replace `$header`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

In `tests/Service/Backup/Dto/BackupHeaderTest.php`, add after the last test method:

```php
        $source = $header->toSource();

        self::assertSame('backup-1202', $source->backupId);
        self::assertSame(4, $source->parts);
        self::assertSame($createdAt, $source->createdAt);
        self::assertSame('https://reader.example', $source->sourceUrl);
        self::assertSame('owner@example.test', $source->sourceEmail);
    }
```

```php
        public \DateTimeImmutable $createdAt,

        public ?string $sourceEmail,
    ) {
    }
}
```


### Task G1: Stateless services are `final readonly`

**Files:**
- Modify: each file named by a `keeps no state, so it is readonly` line (Appendix A-G, 18 at `2cd89be2`), plus `Atom03Parser` and `Atom10Parser`

**Interfaces:** none change. `readonly` on a class with no mutable property changes no behaviour, and PHP refuses the file if a property is written after construction.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add `readonly`**

For each class in the list:
- Replace its declaration `final class <Name>` with `final readonly class <Name>`. For `AbstractAtomParser`, replace `abstract class AbstractAtomParser` with `abstract readonly class AbstractAtomParser`.
- `AbstractAtomParser`'s two subclasses, `Parser/FeedFormatParser/Atom03Parser` and `Atom10Parser`, are not in the report: a class cannot be readonly under a parent that is not, so the rule does not ask for it. PHP requires them to become readonly with their parent, so edit them the same way, by hand.
- In the same file, drop the now-redundant `readonly` from every promoted or declared property: `private readonly ` → `private `, `protected readonly ` → `protected `, `public readonly ` → `public `.

A mechanical helper, run from `backend/`:
```bash
php -r '
foreach (file("var/refactor-1202/services.txt", FILE_IGNORE_NEW_LINES) as $line) {
    if (!preg_match("/^Service role \"rootService\": (App\\\\[\\w\\\\]+) keeps no state, so it is readonly\\.$/", $line, $m)) continue;
    $file = "src/" . str_replace("\\", "/", substr($m[1], 4)) . ".php";
    $short = substr(strrchr("\\" . $m[1], "\\"), 1);
    $code = file_get_contents($file);
    $code = preg_replace("/^(final|abstract) class " . $short . "\\b/m", "\$1 readonly class " . $short, $code, 1, $count);
    if (1 !== $count) { fwrite(STDERR, "No declaration in $file\n"); exit(1); }
    $code = preg_replace("/\\b(private|protected|public) readonly /", "\$1 ", $code);
    file_put_contents($file, $code);
    echo $file, "\n";
}'
```

### Task G2: `InstanceSettings` and `ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl` reset between messages (its own commit, D-P8)

Both memoise a read of the instance-settings row: the row itself, and the public base URL derived from it.
- Under PHP-FPM a request builds a fresh container, so the memo lives for one request.
- The Messenger worker keeps its container. After an admin changes the settings, the worker goes on reading the old row until it restarts. For example, the digest mails it sends link to the old public base URL.

The worker clears the EntityManager after every message, but the memo keeps the detached old row. `ResetInterface` makes the worker drop both memos between messages, which is what their docblocks already promise ("request-scoped"). After each handled message, `Worker::run()` dispatches `WorkerRunningEvent($worker, false)`, and Messenger's `ResetServicesListener` answers it by calling `services_resetter`, which resets every service tagged `kernel.reset`; autoconfiguration tags each `ResetInterface` service so, as it already does for `OwnedTagsCache`.

This is the plan's one behaviour change. It is its own commit, holding only the two classes and their test, and the test goes through that real chain, not through a direct `reset()` call.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Service/Settings/InstanceSettings.php`, `src/Service/Settings/PublicBaseUrl/ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl.php`
- Test: `tests/Service/Settings/SettingsResetBetweenMessagesTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `InstanceSettings implements ResetInterface`, with `reset(): void` (drops the memoised row), and `ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl implements PublicBaseUrlInterface, ResetInterface`, with `reset(): void` (drops the resolved URL).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/Service/Settings/SettingsResetBetweenMessagesTest.php`:
```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Service\Settings;

use App\Entity\InstanceSettingsUpdate;
use App\Service\Settings\InstanceSettings;
use App\Service\Settings\PublicBaseUrl\ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl;
use App\Tests\DbTestCase;
use Symfony\Component\EventDispatcher\EventDispatcherInterface;
use Symfony\Component\Messenger\Event\WorkerRunningEvent;
use Symfony\Component\Messenger\MessageBusInterface;
use Symfony\Component\Messenger\Worker;

/** The worker's view: an admin saves the settings in another process while the worker handles messages (D-P8). */
final class SettingsResetBetweenMessagesTest extends DbTestCase
{
    public function testTheNextMessageSeesAnApprovalSettingSavedElsewhere(): void
    {
        $settings = $this->instanceSettings();
        $settings->update(new InstanceSettingsUpdate(true, false, null, null, null));
        self::assertFalse($settings->requireApproval());

        $this->saveElsewhere('UPDATE instance_setting SET require_approval = 1');
        self::assertFalse($settings->requireApproval(), 'Within one message the memo serves the row it read first.');

        $this->finishAMessage();

        self::assertTrue($settings->requireApproval());
    }

    public function testTheNextMessageLinksToAPublicBaseUrlSavedElsewhere(): void
    {
        $this->instanceSettings()->update(new InstanceSettingsUpdate(false, false, 'https://old.example', null, null));
        /** @var ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl $baseUrl */
        $baseUrl = self::getContainer()->get(ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl::class);
        self::assertSame('https://old.example', $baseUrl->get());

        $this->saveElsewhere("UPDATE instance_setting SET public_base_url = 'https://new.example'");
        self::assertSame('https://old.example', $baseUrl->get(), 'Within one message the URL is memoised.');

        $this->finishAMessage();

        self::assertSame('https://new.example', $baseUrl->get());
    }

    private function instanceSettings(): InstanceSettings
    {
        /** @var InstanceSettings $settings */
        $settings = self::getContainer()->get(InstanceSettings::class);

        return $settings;
    }

    /** Another process's write, which the worker's clear() after each message also leaves the memo holding. */
    private function saveElsewhere(string $statement): void
    {
        $this->em->getConnection()->executeStatement($statement);
        $this->em->clear();
    }

    /** What Worker::run() does after each handled message; the real listener and services_resetter do the rest. */
    private function finishAMessage(): void
    {
        /** @var EventDispatcherInterface $dispatcher */
        $dispatcher = self::getContainer()->get('event_dispatcher');
        $worker = new Worker([], $this->createStub(MessageBusInterface::class), $dispatcher);

        $dispatcher->dispatch(new WorkerRunningEvent($worker, false));
    }
}
```
Each test asserts the stale value inside the first message before it asserts the fresh one after the reset, so neither passes because the memo was never there. `instance_setting`, `require_approval` and `public_base_url` are `InstanceSetting`'s table and columns at `2cd89be2`. The URL test takes `ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl` from the container, because `services_resetter` resets the container's instance, not one the test builds. It is shared by the digest and account mailers, so the test container keeps it; if it reports the id removed or inlined, fetch `PublicBaseUrlInterface::class`, the alias those services inject, instead.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to watch it fail**

Run: `php bin/phpunit tests/Service/Settings/SettingsResetBetweenMessagesTest.php`
Expected: FAIL, twice. `testTheNextMessageSeesAnApprovalSettingSavedElsewhere` fails with `Failed asserting that false is true.`, and `testTheNextMessageLinksToAPublicBaseUrlSavedElsewhere` with `Failed asserting that two strings are identical.` (`-'https://new.example'`, `+'https://old.example'`). Nothing resets either memo yet, which is the leak.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `src/Service/Settings/InstanceSettings.php`:
- Replace `final class InstanceSettings` with `final class InstanceSettings implements ResetInterface`.
- Add `use Symfony\Contracts\Service\ResetInterface;` after `use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;`.
- Add after `update()`:
```php
    public function reset(): void
    {
        $this->memoisedSettings = null;
    }
```
- In the class docblock, replace ` * under PHP-FPM, never promote it to a shared cache. update() clears it so a` with ` * under PHP-FPM and dropped between worker messages by reset(); never promote it to a shared cache. update() clears` and the next line, ` * read after a write sees the new value.`, with ` * it so a read after a write sees the new value.`

In `src/Service/Settings/PublicBaseUrl/ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl.php`:
- Replace `final class ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl implements PublicBaseUrlInterface` with `final class ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl implements PublicBaseUrlInterface, ResetInterface`.
- Add `use Symfony\Contracts\Service\ResetInterface;` after `use Symfony\Component\DependencyInjection\Attribute\Autowire;`.
- Add after `get()`:
```php
    public function reset(): void
    {
        $this->resolved = null;
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run to watch it pass**

Run: `bin/console cache:clear && APP_ENV=test bin/console cache:clear && php bin/phpunit tests/Service/Settings tests/Service/Mail/Digest`
Expected: PASS. `InstanceSettingsTest::testResolvesTheRowOnceAcrossSeveralGetters` still passes: a reset happens between messages, not between getters.

- [ ] **Step 5: The container resets them**

```bash
bin/console debug:container --tag=kernel.reset --format=txt | grep -E "InstanceSettings|ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl"
bin/console debug:event-dispatcher "Symfony\Component\Messenger\Event\WorkerRunningEvent" | grep -i reset
```

One at a time, restoring each by hand, and clearing the test container after each edit (`APP_ENV=test bin/console cache:clear`), since the tag comes from the compiled container:
1. In `InstanceSettings`, replace `final class InstanceSettings implements ResetInterface` with `final class InstanceSettings`, keeping `reset()`. Expected: `testTheNextMessageSeesAnApprovalSettingSavedElsewhere` fails with `Failed asserting that false is true.` Without the interface nothing tags the service, so nothing resets it, whatever `reset()` says. (The URL test fails too: it reads through the stale settings.)

### Task G3: `TickLockKeepalive` and `SweepStreamHeartbeat` reset between messages

Both are armed for one unit of work: a tick, a sweep. Their only callers disarm them in a `finally`, so nothing leaks today. The state still belongs to one message, so `ResetInterface` is the true declaration, and `reset()` is the disarm the callers already call.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/TickLockKeepalive.php`, `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/SweepStreamHeartbeat.php` (#1161 A2 moved it here)
- Test: `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/TickLockKeepaliveTest.php`, `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/SweepStreamHeartbeatTest.php` (one test each)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `TickLockKeepalive implements CompletionStreamHeartbeatInterface, ResetInterface`, whose `reset()` calls `release()`. `SweepStreamHeartbeat implements CompletionStreamHeartbeatInterface, ResetInterface`, whose `reset()` calls `sweepEnded()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

In `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/TickLockKeepaliveTest.php`, add after `testTheFirstBeatAfterHoldRefreshes()`:
```php
    public function testAResetBetweenMessagesDisarmsIt(): void
    {
        $clock = new MockClock('2026-08-16 12:00:00');
        $keepalive = new TickLockKeepalive($clock, new Logger('test'));
        $lock = new RefreshCountingLock();
        $keepalive->hold($lock, self::LOCK_RESOURCE);

        $keepalive->reset();
        $keepalive->beat();

        self::assertSame(0, $lock->refreshCount());
    }
```

In `tests/Service/Recommendation/Run/SweepStreamHeartbeatTest.php`, add after `testItMarksTheSweepingKindOnTheFirstBeat()`:
```php
    public function testAResetBetweenMessagesDisarmsIt(): void
    {
        $clock = new MockClock('2026-08-16 12:00:00');
        $heartbeat = new SweepStreamHeartbeat($this->presence($clock), $clock);
        $heartbeat->sweepStarted(RecommendationDriverKind::PersistentWorker);

        $heartbeat->reset();
        $heartbeat->beat();

        self::assertNull($this->touchedAt(RecommendationDriverKind::PersistentWorker));
    }
```
`testTheFirstBeatAfterHoldRefreshes` and `testItMarksTheSweepingKindOnTheFirstBeat` prove that an armed first beat writes. The new tests differ from them only by the reset.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to watch them fail**

Run: `php bin/phpunit --filter testAResetBetweenMessagesDisarmsIt tests/Service/Recommendation/Run`
Expected: FAIL with `Call to undefined method …::reset()`, twice.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/TickLockKeepalive.php`:
- Replace `final class TickLockKeepalive implements CompletionStreamHeartbeatInterface` with `final class TickLockKeepalive implements CompletionStreamHeartbeatInterface, ResetInterface`.
- Add `use Symfony\Contracts\Service\ResetInterface;` in order.
- Add after `release()`:
```php
    public function reset(): void
    {
        $this->release();
    }
```

In `src/Service/Recommendation/Run/SweepStreamHeartbeat.php`:
- Replace `final class SweepStreamHeartbeat implements CompletionStreamHeartbeatInterface` with `final class SweepStreamHeartbeat implements CompletionStreamHeartbeatInterface, ResetInterface`.
- Add `use Symfony\Contracts\Service\ResetInterface;` in order.
- Add after `sweepEnded()`:
```php
    public function reset(): void
    {
        $this->sweepEnded();
    }
```

Expected: PASS. `CompletionStreamHeartbeatWiringTest` still finds both members of the composite.

### Task G4: `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` on the seven process-lifetime services; enforce

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `src/Service/Grafana/EffectiveGrafanaSettings.php`
  - `src/Service/Search/EntryIndexer.php`
  - `src/Service/Logging/RequestIdProvider.php`
  - `src/Service/Mail/DeferredMailer.php`
  - `src/Service/Mail/Transport/DynamicMailTransport.php`
  - `src/Service/Passkey/PasskeyCeremony.php`
  - `src/Service/Profiling/ProfileSampler/ExcimerSampler.php`

- [ ] **Step 1: The markers**

Add `use App\DependencyInjection\ProcessLifetimeState;` in order to each file. Put the attribute on its own line directly above the `final class` line, after the class docblock:

| Class | Attribute |
|---|---|
| `EffectiveGrafanaSettings` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('A per-process memo over the shared cache; refresh() and forgetStored() renew it (#1159)')]` |
| `EntryIndexer` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('The index settings are pushed once per process')]` |
| `RequestIdProvider` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('RequestIdListener starts a new id for every request and every worker message')]` |
| `DeferredMailer` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('Drained on kernel.terminate; a reset between messages would drop queued mail')]` |
| `DynamicMailTransport` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('The transport is cached per settings signature, re-checked on every send')]` |
| `PasskeyCeremony` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('Library machinery built from no setting; host() reads the relying party each call')]` |
| `ExcimerSampler` | `#[ProcessLifetimeState('WorkerProfilingListener samples the worker across messages')]` |

Each reason says why the state may outlive a message, in one line. No attribute line exceeds 120 columns.

Dispatch one fresh reviewer subagent with `model: "opus"`, this plan, the issue and `git diff origin/develop...HEAD`. It reports findings; it does not fix. Attack points:
1. **Each verdict in "Stateful services".** For each `#[ProcessLifetimeState]` service, can the state go stale for the worker the way `InstanceSettings`' did? The reason must hold in the worker, not only under PHP-FPM.
2. **The resets are safe.** A `kernel.reset` between messages drops nothing a later message needs: no queued mail and no held lock. `TickLockKeepalive::reset()` keeps `lockLost`, as `release()` does.
3. **`readonly`** changed no behaviour: the suite is green, and no class in the list keeps a lazily built field.
4. **No closing keyword** in any commit message.
5. **Deletion checks.** Re-run one of G2's two checks (a `ResetInterface` removed) and quote its FAIL; G2 is its own commit, holding only the two resets and their test.

```bash
gh pr create --base develop --title "refactor(#1202): services are final readonly; stateful ones reset between messages or say why not" --body "$(cat <<'BODY'
Refs #1202 (PR G of nine).

- Twenty stateless services become `final readonly` (eighteen the rule named, plus the two Atom parsers that inherit from one of them).
- Four stateful services now implement `ResetInterface`, so the Messenger worker drops their state between messages.
  - **Behaviour change:** `InstanceSettings` and `ConfiguredPublicBaseUrl` used to keep the settings row and the public base URL they first read until the worker restarted. The worker now sees an admin's change at its next message. For example, digest mails now link to the current public base URL.
  - `TickLockKeepalive` and `SweepStreamHeartbeat` were already disarmed by their callers. `reset()` makes that the declared contract.
- Seven services whose state outlives a message on purpose carry `#[ProcessLifetimeState('why')]`.
- `rootService` and `statefulService` are now enforced.

No wire change. After deploying, the worker must be restarted to pick up the change, as after any release.
BODY
)"
gh pr view --json body --jq .body | grep -inE '\b(close|fix|resolve)[sd]?\b|\b(closed|fixed|fixes|resolves|resolved|closes)\b' && echo 'STOP: closing keyword in the body' || true
```
Expected: neither `STOP` line prints.

```bash
Refs #1202 (PR H of nine).

- `NotifyAdminsOfPendingApproval`, `InsecureProductionConfigGuard`, `AddUserIdClaimOnTokenIssue` and `StampLastLoginOnTokenIssue` take the `Listener` suffix. Their tests follow them.
- The worker's handlers already carry their message's name plus `Handler`, and its messages are imperative with no suffix.
- `listenerName` and `handlerName` are now enforced.

No behaviour change and no wire change. The listeners register through their attributes, as before.
BODY
)"
gh pr view --json body --jq .body | grep -inE '\b(close|fix|resolve)[sd]?\b|\b(closed|fixed|fixes|resolves|resolved|closes)\b' && echo 'STOP: closing keyword in the body' || true
```
Expected: neither `STOP` line prints.

```bash
Closes #1202 (PR I of nine; PRs A to H are merged).

- `ServiceRoleRule` now reports every check, and a new class outside its role fails `composer stan` and names its home.
- The report-only wiring is gone: the `serviceRoleChecks` parameter, `phpstan-service-roles.neon` and `composer roles`.
- CLAUDE.md drops its transitional wording.

Over the nine PRs, every class in `src/Service` and `src/Http` has one role, and its folder and its name say which:
- services in the module root, `final readonly`, or stateful with `ResetInterface` or `#[ProcessLifetimeState]`;
- models in `Model/` with the `Model` suffix;
- DTOs in `Dto/`;
- factories in `Factory/` with the `Factory` suffix, with the build-and-save services split;
- per-call objects in `Pass/`;
- static helpers in `Support/`;
- each interface in a folder named after it, with its implementations;
- listeners ending in `Listener`.

No behaviour change and no wire change in this PR.
BODY
)"
```
